## 2026.11.10


### OpenClaw v2026.11.10

For formatted release notes, [read this release on the docs site](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.11.10).

**Release scale:** 7,671 pull requests, 633 direct commits, and 349 contributors.

#### Highlights

##### Deep Research

Investigate complex questions across multiple web sources or local documents without leaving your chat, returning a cited report with side-by-side quotes when sources disagree and explicit gaps where evidence is missing. Deep Research is **enabled by default** out of the box on the portable engine using your existing model and web tools (with Google's managed research engine available as an opt-in), so you can simply ask your agent to run deep research in chat. You can walk away while it works, rely on eligible jobs resuming across Gateway restarts, and revisit saved reports for seven days. Learn how it works and see prompt examples in the [Deep Research guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/deep-research).
When sources conflict, the portable engine surfaces quoted passages side by side and marks open questions under **Unresolved** so you can verify claims directly instead of trusting a summary. Supply local files or URLs alongside web search, follow live progress in chat, or switch to the opt-in Google managed engine in plugin settings.

[#173964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173964), [#177232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177232), [#173969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173969), [#176887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176887).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

##### Knowledge

Turn scattered conversations, meetings, notes, and files into a linked local Markdown vault of people, projects, decisions, and supporting excerpts you can browse in the Control UI explorer and trace on project Timelines. Knowledge is **opt-in** and takes one setting to enable (`plugins.slots.memory` set to `"knowledge"`, or selecting **Knowledge** as your memory provider in Settings), hot-reloading without a Gateway restart while leaving your existing `memory-core` data untouched. Instead of digging through old threads to remember why a call was made, you can trace decisions straight back to the source, let scheduled scribes keep pages current from conversations or Granola meetings, and revise or merge claims without erasing history. See the [Knowledge guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/knowledge) for setup, scribes, search, and everyday workflows.
**Capture and curate from real work.** Hand your agent a document, article, or conversation to record decisions and commitments with retained excerpts, or run built-in scribes to ingest OpenClaw conversations, Granola meetings, and existing memory notes or Dreams diaries without altering the originals.

**Search, recall, and correct safely.** Search by keyword, alias, or date in the Control UI explorer, open a project's Timeline, or enable optional automatic recall (starting in shadow mode so you can inspect matches first; note that keyword scoring in shadow mode sends your message and candidate excerpts to the configured Decision provider). Revisions, withdrawals, and merges keep earlier versions inspectable with local Git checkpoints, and stores can stay per-agent or be shared across agents.

[#180139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180139), [#187676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187676), [#199744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199744).

Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

[#193742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193742).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).

[#188057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188057).

Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

##### Browser handoff

Step directly into an agent-controlled browser tab whenever a task hits a sign-in wall, 2FA prompt, CAPTCHA, or sensitive form you would rather complete yourself. Browser handoff is **ready to use** on any supported Gateway-hosted Browser tab in the Control UI Browser panel: click **Take control** to pause the agent's clicks and typing while you finish the step, then click **Release control** (or let control return automatically after five minutes without input). You avoid aborting long runs or losing page state while keeping your agent moving right where you left off. See [Browser handoff](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser/profiles#browser-handoff) for details and private Chrome settings access.
Taking control pauses automated clicks and typing on that browser tab while keeping screenshots, snapshots, and work on other tabs available. You can also open Chrome settings and the password manager privately in the Browser panel without exposing them to agents or other clients.

[#197928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197928).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).

#### Installation and Onboarding

Quick Start now keeps the Gateway running in the background after you close the terminal, and adding your first model provider verifies the key immediately and takes you straight into a working chat.



##### Quick Start background service and npx setup

  Quick Start now offers to keep the Gateway running as a background service instead of stopping when you close the onboarding terminal. If you try OpenClaw through `npx` or `dlx`, the installed background service stays runnable after package-runner cache cleanups, and the closing next steps print commands you can run without a global `openclaw` install.
[#191454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191454), [#192331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192331).

##### First-provider setup and API key verification

  Saving or replacing an API key in Settings -> Models now checks the key right away instead of waiting for a failed reply in chat. When the provider accepts the key, the form confirms verification and closes. When the provider rejects the key for authentication or billing reasons, the form stays open with the reason so you can paste a different key.

On a fresh install with no usable model yet, saving your first provider activates it immediately and ends with a direct action to start a new chat with that model.
[#193507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193507), [#193892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193892).

#### Web UI

Control UI receives a cleaner tool-call and reasoning timeline, faster background session launches from **Cmd+K** and New Session, a redesigned Dashboards gallery and Settings layout, and Catalan language support.



##### Chat activity timeline, inline command trail, and composer

  Tool activity in Control UI chat now reads as a cleaner timeline. Consecutive launches, stops, reads, and commands share compact rows with factual summaries, and each settled subagent keeps its own completion, timeout, or failure row live and after reload. With `/reasoning on`, reasoning steps appear as quiet one-line rows instead of large dashed boxes.

If you prefer watching shell commands, file reads, and searches unfold in conversation order, choose **View -> Inline command trail (this browser)** in the session menu. Command output advances in place, and the preference stays saved in that browser for that Gateway.
[#198133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198133).

##### Command palette, background sessions, and sidebar

  You can launch sessions in the background without waiting for slow worktree or worker preparation. Press **Cmd/Ctrl+Enter** in New Session or **Cmd+K** to send a prompt in the background while keeping your current place, start several **Cmd+K** sessions concurrently, and preview a starting session on its route before the Gateway finishes creating it. **Cmd+K** Compose also gains a model picker.

In the sidebar, older sessions load automatically as you scroll to the bottom, you can create empty nested sessions directly from the sidebar, double-click a session name to rename it inline, and show a scannable QR code beside public **Anyone with the link** URLs.
[#197481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197481), [#190417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190417), [#190451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190451), [#190465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190465), [#198316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198316).

##### Model picker, agent controls, and Usage

  You can star favorite models per agent directly inside the chat model picker without closing the menu, and models in provider cooldown now show their cooldown status while staying disabled. On the Agents page, each agent's utility model can now be viewed and edited without opening raw config, and **Settings -> Talk** adds a picker for the speech-to-text model.

On the Usage page, filters move into a single searchable **Filter** menu with an active chips row, and the legacy **Activity by time** chart is removed.
[#180824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180824).

##### Dashboards gallery, Files, and plugin surfaces

  The Dashboards gallery has been redesigned with framed cards, fading long titles, an inline search trigger, a multi-select author filter, and a **Display** menu for switching between grid and list views, ordering, and grouping.

Across workspace panels, session files can now be tiled and sorted in the Files panel, hovering a session automation shows its next run time, plugin details group UI contributions under **Extensions** with distinct capability and custom panel icons, and the GitHub publication account picker displays account avatars.

##### Settings redesign, Catalan locale, mobile web, and performance

  Settings pages across Control UI have been rebuilt around cleaner layouts, including Gateway, About, Appearance, Logs, and MCP server cards. **Connected apps** moves into **Settings -> Agents & tools**, operators can dismiss the read-only Gateway banner per browser, and **Català** joins **Settings -> Appearance -> Language**.

On phones, bottom sheets now use spring physics with swipe-to-dismiss, settings subpages add back navigation and **Esc** up-navigation, and phone layouts tighten across Approvals, Logs, Channels, and Automations.

##### Agent Templates in Labs

  Agent Templates remains behind **Settings -> Labs -> Templates** and is off by default. With the lab enabled, you can add agents directly from published ClawHub packages in the CLI or Control UI, preview recommended templates in a compact dialog without leaving chat, and start drafting in an editable pending chat while creation and a retryable setup turn finish.

Templates can now install bundled plugins through native plugin management, recommend one exact ClawHub plugin card during setup, and start declared automations paused until onboarding enables them.
[#195707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195707), [#196395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196395), [#198070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198070), [#198603](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198603), [#193778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193778), [#196349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196349), [#198065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198065), [#196323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196323).

##### OpenClaw ID remote access in Labs

  OpenClaw ID remote access remains behind **Settings -> Labs -> OpenClaw ID remote access** and is off by default. Owners now receive alerts in the Control UI when an invited member or node is waiting for approval, and Control UI adds key rotation and key-health warnings for linked claws.

Invited members without a profile display name are named from their email address and prompted for their name on first visit, and clicking **Link** in **Settings -> Profile** reuses a still-valid OpenClaw ID sign-in instead of asking for a second device code.
[#199719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199719), [#196456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196456), [#198491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198491), [#198323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198323).

Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Stops a linked claw from silently minting new keys when key files are missing, preserves owner approvals across reconnects, and reconnects returning OpenClaw ID browser tabs automatically.
  [#196380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196380).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  [f69df8e7c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f69df8e7ce078015f3dde3b8eb4aaf38596ac524).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#197011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197011).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Shows the Access page when OpenClaw ID is enabled with a plugin allowlist, fixes blank script widgets for OpenClaw ID members, exposes device approval in the member account menu, and reconciles remote-access relay links.
  [#201556](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201556), [#198459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198459), [#199354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199354), [#198344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198344), [#198474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198474).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

#### Updates and Maintenance

Automatic updates can now run on a chosen schedule, package updates run faster and recover more reliably from interrupted installs, and PostgreSQL setups gain online backups, staging restore drills, and multi-core archive compression.



##### Automatic update schedules

  Managed Gateways with automatic updates enabled can now choose when update campaigns start. In **Settings -> Updates -> Update schedule**, pick **Hourly (default)**, **Daily**, **Weekly**, or **Custom cron**, or set `update.auto.schedule` in config. Times follow the Gateway host's timezone and apply without restarting the Gateway, while keeping **Hold 1 h**, manual **Update Gateway**, and idle-restart rules unchanged.
[#193713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193713).

Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Shows newly published releases right away instead of waiting for a second daily check.
  [#181949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181949).

  Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).



##### Faster, safer updates and interrupted-update recovery

  Pre-activation checks are much faster on large installations because the updater replaces its full scratch-copy canary rehearsal with a stateless runtime contract check, building on candidate-owned rehearsal support. On OpenClaw-owned package installs, each release now lives in its own directory behind a linked package root.

State upgrades at Gateway startup now copy databases into a private shadow, migrate and verify integrity and row counts there, and atomically swap them into place while keeping the prior generation as a fallback. Successful updates also clean up old pre-update copies instead of accumulating them, and interactive updates offer to remove retirable recovery copies when disk space is tight.
[#198268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198268).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

##### Backups and PostgreSQL operations

  PostgreSQL installations can now create routine backup archives while the Gateway stays running using per-store repeatable-read export snapshots. You can also take lightweight `--local-companion` checkpoints without exporting live database rows, run staging restore drills with `--postgres-connection-env`, and opt in to server-side PostgreSQL rollback schemas (`database.postgres.recoveryCaptures: "server"`) instead of exporting full local SQLite files during updates and Doctor.

Backup archives now compress across multiple CPU cores and report stage and byte progress on long runs. New flags let you capture exact SQLite migration capsules or omit regenerable worktree templates (`--exclude-worktree-templates`) and Doctor migration originals (`--exclude-migration-originals`).

  [#199781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199781).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  [#199850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199850), [#199869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199869).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Fixes moving agents back from PostgreSQL to SQLite after updates, avoids false schema-drift refusals on pre-Template PostgreSQL stores, restores PostgreSQL model billing warnings, and lets macOS start with ready credentials when a PostgreSQL secret is unavailable.
  [#200383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200383).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).

  [#197425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197425).

  Thanks [@ceckert](https://github.com/ceckert).

  [#202050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202050), [#202102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202102).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).



##### Doctor and service recovery

  Doctor can now convert legacy copied model allowlists into provider wildcards so newly added models from your configured providers are no longer hidden by default. Routine Doctor and update inspections also stop making unnecessary full copies of the live state database and reclaim leftover snapshot copies.
[#190031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190031).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[#193648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193648).

Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).

[#194042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194042), [#194267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194267), [#197277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197277).

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

[#193229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193229).

Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Fixes Doctor repairs when the state directory is a symlink, on btrfs NOCOW volumes, on disabled Windows Gateway tasks, with case-differing agent directories, with stale macOS lock files, or with lingering Codex app-server plugin paths and old Matrix heartbeat results.
  [#188560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188560).

  Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).

  [#201912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201912).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [08fe0cd5a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/08fe0cd5a6eac935b04e73680440a99747d00e29).

  Thanks [@Satoshi-Kusumoto](https://github.com/Satoshi-Kusumoto), [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#202651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202651).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Fixes Doctor leaving literal quotes in macOS service credentials, hanging on a leaked handle after repair, stalling on heartbeat migrations after a full disk, failing non-interactive runs on advisory plugin warnings, or staying silent when provider catalog discovery fails, and improves split Node and systemd recovery diagnostics.
  [#189482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189482).

  [#194229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194229).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

##### Gateway lifecycle, startup, and service updates

  - Fixes updates from 2026.9.x releases falsely reporting post-update failure even though the new release installed and the Gateway is healthy.
    [#198429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198429).

    Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Retries temporary SQLite worker capacity failures during Gateway startup so affected agents come up cleanly instead of staying degraded.
  [#173994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173994).

  Thanks [@McgAtom](https://github.com/McgAtom).
- Reports leftover plaintext archive caches in openclaw doctor --lint and removes them with openclaw doctor --fix.
  [#193608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193608).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Omits changing Codex diagnostic SQLite logs from local-companion restart checkpoints while keeping state, credentials, and full backups intact.
  [#190823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190823).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Allows backup maintenance and openclaw doctor --fix to reclaim abandoned verification scratch directories left behind after a crash.
  [#191081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191081).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reuses the verified clean-close seal on Gateway restarts to avoid redundant full database integrity scans.
  [#195112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195112).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shows the specific invalid `--agent` option conflict message in `openclaw status` human output instead of a generic CLI failure notice.
  [#196081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196081).

  Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Fixes `openclaw gateway restart` and update verification waiting out the full retry budget on structured Gateway auth rejections such as scope mismatches instead of reporting auth rejection immediately.
  [#196426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196426).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Stops status, channels list, and doctor commands from printing Matrix SDK deprecation warnings when Matrix is not configured.
  [#190257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190257).

  Thanks [@jerome-queck](https://github.com/jerome-queck).
- Fixes removed config environment values or legacy filesystem modes persisting after worker startup on stock Bun.
  [#195153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195153).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses cached fonts, themes, and icons across Gateway updates whenever the bundled public assets have not changed.
  [#194718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194718).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recognizes completed runtime-pin migrations across path aliases so already-migrated daemons are not re-migrated.
  [`774b7f5ee4`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/774b7f5ee4dccbdd9979647a482629ca6c345fda).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Prevents PostgreSQL generation hashing during updates and Doctor checks from spilling huge temporary sort files to disk.
  [#187839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187839).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Rejects incompatible SQLite port ownership settings before modifying stores and explains how to recover an interrupted port first.
  [`1ce4331ed5`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1ce4331ed5d11d58f4d352ea624778b770dd64bd).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Keeps Gateway suspension renewals alive while capturing large SQLite migration archives so captures do not time out.
  [#190809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190809).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Allows external controllers to capture an exact SQLite migration archive from an already-suspended local Gateway without stopping it.
  [#189802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189802).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Adds openclaw database restore-session-dependencies to repair selected session skill revisions and repository checkpoints while the Gateway is stopped.
  [#190833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190833).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Reclaims abandoned read-only database snapshots after 15 minutes and avoids copying the full state database during plain openclaw status reads.
  [#192368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192368).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes repeated agent-database verification after restarts when native retirement revoked an earlier proof, and verifies runtime stores absent from the startup roster.
  [#198620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198620).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Defers old release pruning until after deployment cutover and verification complete so cleanup does not stall the serving Gateway before builds.
  [#198624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198624).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stops Gateway logs from repeating the same withheld-command warning every time a node reconnects with an unchanged command policy.
  [#190021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190021).

  Thanks [@scs0209](https://github.com/scs0209).
- Registers new SQLite cron repair backups in the recovery cleanup inventory so they follow the standard cleanup lifecycle.
  [#190328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190328).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes Doctor and updates repeating full integrity scans on large agent databases when post-migration opens lack a reusable clean-close seal.
  [#189247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189247).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Doctor becoming unresponsive while inspecting large Codex native-assignment inventories.
  [#189248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189248).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes failed standalone Doctor recovery captures leaving unsealed copies behind instead of cleaning them up once backup work settles.
  [#189249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189249).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes repeated startup warnings and unrepairable Doctor deferrals for historical archived or finished sessions with unmatched legacy restart custody.
  [#189261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189261).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes invalid read-only configuration errors telling operators to run doctor or validate commands that cannot edit an externally managed config.
  [#189531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189531).

  Thanks [@freeqaz](https://github.com/freeqaz).
- Fixes Doctor rejecting legacy auth-profile migrations on PostgreSQL stores with a false backup mismatch error.
  [#189639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189639).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes recurring Doctor legacy-delivery warnings when a native status command is the first reply after starting a new session.
  [#190213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190213).
- Improves Doctor warnings when an earlier migration refusal blocked workspace import, naming the blocking migration instead of telling you to restore from backup.
  [#191442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191442).

  Thanks [@BitmapAsset](https://github.com/BitmapAsset).
- Stops `openclaw doctor --non-interactive` and redirected-stdin Doctor runs from downloading provider plugin packages without explicit `--fix`, `--repair`, or `--yes` consent.
  [#191523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191523).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@brandonulrich](https://github.com/brandonulrich).
- Avoids copying cleanly closed SQLite databases during offline Doctor reclamation checks, speeding up maintenance on large stores.
  [#191568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191568).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reads committed Gateway owner leases directly through a read-only connection instead of copying the entire state database when the recorded owner is dead or unverifiable.
  [#191639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191639).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reads SQLite headers directly during Doctor database-size checks when no active WAL is present, avoiding multi-gigabyte snapshot copies.
  [#191835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191835).

  Thanks [@JordanNewell](https://github.com/JordanNewell) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates Linux Doctor checks to warn when native child reaping is unavailable and explain that supervised launches can fail until a compatible native process helper is restored.
  [#191915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191915).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
- Allows Doctor to clear no-op migration warnings for disabled plugins when no source data exists, without enabling the plugin.
  [#190441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190441).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw doctor --fix failing to create a pre-repair recovery copy when an installation still has an unmigrated legacy memory index.
  [#199654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199654).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw doctor --fix on Linux leaving abandoned updater runtime directories behind when unrelated root-owned Node processes are running.
  [#200657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200657).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Doctor legacy-state migration using a stale launch-time home directory after HOME changes.
  [#202850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202850).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes `openclaw doctor` on macOS reporting healthy when the stale-updater launchd scan fails instead of surfacing a warning.
  [#195744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195744).

  Thanks [@shuisadev](https://github.com/shuisadev).
- Fixes `openclaw doctor` reporting a clean result when the `agents/` directory cannot be read, surfacing a warning instead of silently skipping orphan-directory checks.
  [#195845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195845).

  Thanks [@shuisadev](https://github.com/shuisadev) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes `openclaw gateway status --deep` on macOS reporting healthy when the stale-updater launchd scan fails instead of printing a warning.
  [#195977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195977).

  Thanks [@shuisadev](https://github.com/shuisadev).
- Fixes `openclaw doctor` silently reporting clean when the agent directory cannot be read during the stale OAuth shadow scan, printing a warning with permissions guidance instead.
  [#196465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196465).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Increases the standalone openclaw doctor original-state backup timeout from 10 to 20 minutes so verified backups can finish on slow storage.
  [#192406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192406).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Points directly to the failing config file path when Doctor reports configuration validation errors.
  [`5258427907`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5258427907b1b4b62586a364800bdac243110fde).

  Thanks [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Restores `openclaw doctor` detection of immutable or unwritable state databases on macOS even when no migration is pending, including the `chflags nouchg` fix.
  [#199509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199509).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw doctor leaving no-op plugin state migrations pending when a plugin is enabled, its channel is disabled, and no retired data exists.
  [#194897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194897).
- Fixes openclaw doctor reporting an unknown Logbook schema when running through compiled workers.
  [#192370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192370).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Fixes openclaw doctor and updates deleting working CLI-runtime model pins and fallbacks when models.mode is set to replace.
  [#197412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197412).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses read-only SQLite inspection snapshots across Doctor checks instead of repeatedly copying and rereading the database on every check.
  [#188030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188030).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prints the failing operation, target path, and writable-storage guidance when Doctor hits a read-only container filesystem without requiring debug mode.
  [#188331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188331).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prints clear recovery steps as soon as Doctor pauses a managed Gateway and whenever repair admission, cleanup, or restoration fails.
  [#188495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188495).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes plugin Doctor migrations failing while paging existing plugin state on PostgreSQL due to NUL-padded pagination cursors.
  [#188693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188693).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Enforces a ten-minute capture deadline on stalled Doctor pre-repair backup inventories and restores the stopped Gateway service if capture fails.
  [#189025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189025).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@hbgangcn1](https://github.com/hbgangcn1).
- Eliminates false unknown-migration recovery warnings during Doctor and updates for Discord's disposable command cache and LINE's core-captured queue rows.
  [#189031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189031).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Speeds up Doctor cold-navigation backfill across large archived-session databases while preserving identical navigation data.
  [#189050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189050).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Doctor aborting when inspecting pending state migrations for a disabled external plugin.
  [#193261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193261).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes post-update Doctor aborting on configured external channels before legacy-state repairs can finish.
  [#193301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193301).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reconciles stale Doctor rollback receipts onto surviving byte-verified duplicate archives so missing-archive warnings do not repeat forever.
  [#190652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190652).
- Skips hashing unrelated or incomplete retained recovery copies in openclaw doctor before rejecting them.
  [#192407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192407).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids blocking signal handling during Doctor SQLite snapshot preparation on large databases and recovers same-boot database leases.
  [#190788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190788).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Makes Doctor session and shared-auth database inspection cancellable while preparing inspection snapshots.
  [#190870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190870).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Makes Doctor process and restart lease inspection cancellable and ensures offline inspection follows replaced state files.
  [#190872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190872).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Awaits native credential migration observations outside transactions so Doctor does not block cancellation.
  [#190873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190873).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows cancelling Doctor cron and daemon SQLite snapshot preparation cleanly with temporary file cleanup.
  [#190874](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190874).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Makes Doctor shared-state repair snapshot preparation cancellable while preserving existing repair guarantees.
  [#190875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190875).

  Thanks [@chrislro](https://github.com/chrislro) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repairs legacy ISO-8601 session end timestamps during openclaw doctor checks instead of blocking updates.
  [#194685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194685).
- Adds repair support in openclaw doctor --fix to fetch complete commit history for legacy shallow managed project clones while keeping blobless filtering.
  [#194476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194476).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents post-plugin Doctor from widening into a full repair pass when settling a plugin migration without changing config.
  [#193476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193476).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps standing custom Codex command and Computer Use advisories from forcing a full second Doctor pass during updates.
  [#193559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193559).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps standing Codex request-parameter advice from triggering a full second Doctor pass and reports all config blockers in one run.
  [#193650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193650).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw doctor failing during v16 agent-database upgrades when a database already includes OpenClaw's lazy memory full-text search index.
  [#194359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194359).

  Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star).
- Speeds up the second Doctor pass during native updates by running only the six required checks when deferred model retirement is the sole remaining step.
  [#186231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186231).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Adds batched multi-window support to `diagnostics.cpuProfile` so consecutive production CPU profiles start and stop the profiler once instead of pausing the Gateway repeatedly.
  [#196422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196422).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Raises trajectory prompt capture and export limits to 65,536 UTF-16 units so larger compiled prompts are not silently truncated to short previews while keeping secrets and local paths redacted.
  [#196606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196606).

  Thanks [@lastguru-net](https://github.com/lastguru-net).
- Saves batched and oversized `diagnostics.cpuProfile` captures to a private local `.cpuprofile` file with a summary instead of discarding them when they exceed RPC response limits.
  [#196652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196652).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes missing Prometheus byte totals for large session actor receipts when serialization size changes across the worker boundary.
  [#199857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199857).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Adds correlation identifiers to oversized workspace recovery journal warnings so operators can match retries to reconciliation attempts.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Prometheus metrics omitting active series when large unused family reservations starve smaller metric families under the aggregate cap.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#187393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187393).

  [#198148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198148).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses fresh package-manager ownership discovery during Gateway-triggered updates instead of running discovery twice.
  [#190592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190592).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Stops openclaw update before activation when a running node host service holds the shared state database open.
  [#203578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203578).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Records failed shell completion or service definition refreshes during updates so they are reported in JSON output and can be retried automatically.
  [#200331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200331).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Automatically recovers a managed update and restarts the Gateway when the updater process is killed mid-update after reserving a plugin cohort, without requiring manual repair.
  [#191201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191201).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reports the actual exit code or signal when a migrated update finalize worker exits early instead of a misleading missing `result.json` error.
  [#191676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191676).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes candidate Doctor failing exec-approvals migration with a database-locked error during upgrades from 2026.10.10.
  [#191772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191772).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Continues through Doctor instead of failing an update when the updater cannot read the ledger write generation before Doctor starts.
  [#193622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193622).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Distinguishes a recovered Gateway from failed update checks and keeps recovered update outcomes visible in the Inbox.
  [`a76790baaa`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a76790baaa6c147ba010912dcaad3119937fdb6f).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Keeps the Gateway watching an active update run across transient ledger scan failures so progress and completion notices are not lost.
  [#188015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188015).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Prevents detached managed update recovery from overwriting unrelated restart notices or borrowing failure reasons from another installation.
  [#188212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188212).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Verifies exact macOS launchd job identity during managed update handoff so unrelated jobs cannot be mistaken for the selected service.
  [#188226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188226).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Treats automatic update checks without a managed service handoff as skipped with manual-update guidance instead of triggering failure triage.
  [#188405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188405).
- Stops failed-update triage for custom state directories or config paths from probing an unrelated native Gateway service on the host.
  [#188879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188879).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Caps Doctor archive cold-navigation backfill to about ten minutes per run so large transcript libraries do not keep updating Gateways offline.
  [#189039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189039).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Doctor getting blocked on a SQLite migration lock when retrying an update from a restored package rollback.
  [`24d075dbfa`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/24d075dbfad9bf4495a9ae4c4ece3da711c47fe3).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes `openclaw update status` timing out immediately and dropping available status details when passed a very large `--timeout` allowance.
  [#196045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196045).

  Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Fails fast before candidate validation in `openclaw update` when a running PostgreSQL Gateway holds state ownership that the updater will not stop.
  [#196157](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196157).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Fixes generated macOS update notes stripping angle-bracket command placeholders such as `<job-id>` inside inline code spans.
  [#196438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196438).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
- Keeps retired SQLite schema catalog hashes resolvable across schema edits so installed updaters do not fail during in-place build swaps.
  [#196800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196800).

  Thanks [@Devin-Linux](https://github.com/Devin-Linux), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes old updater runtimes piling up on Linux and Windows because openclaw update and openclaw doctor --fix could not reclaim them while the Gateway was running.
  [#197833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197833).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Records the post-update backup cleanup step in update results so long cleanup waits are visible in update status and JSON output.
  [#189472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189472).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Windows updates leaving the Gateway stopped after a failed post-update verification by re-enabling its Scheduled Task and retaining recovery until readiness is verified.
  [#189647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189647).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unifies update recovery capture tracking so update status provides an exact cleanup preview command and lets confirmed cleanup retire stale historical captures.
  [#189657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189657).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes generated update failure reports dropping recorded exit codes and failure details when a restart sentinel has a null exit code.
  [#189963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189963).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes upgrades from older OpenClaw releases failing with false SQLite migration source-changed errors during Doctor setup.
  [#190179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190179).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reports an explicit unknown task state and diagnostic when Windows denies launching PowerShell during Gateway task inspection.
  [#190464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190464).
- Fixes Status and Doctor repeatedly reporting false service drift warnings on Windows when Task Scheduler exports UseUnifiedSchedulingEngine.
  [#189882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189882).

  Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn).
- Fixes SQLite snapshot reads and Doctor migration detection failing in Windows EFS-encrypted cache directories.
  [#190087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190087).
- Fixes Windows process identity checks expiring early or running past their timeout when the system clock changes.
  [#190236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190236).
- Reclaims leftover openclaw-update-runtime directories on Windows during openclaw doctor --fix and later updates.
  [#192129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192129).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes failed Windows updates leaving the Gateway Scheduled Task disabled during rollback so the Gateway restarts automatically.
  [#203264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203264).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes read-only database connections from status readers or backup tools causing Windows updates to fail during Doctor checks.
  [#204095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/204095).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Moves large managed-worktree archive preparation off the Gateway main thread to avoid UI and server stalls during cleanup.
  [#192358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192358).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes overlapping worktree cleanup waits cancelling each other's scheduler wakeup and leaving cleanup pending until shutdown.
  [#197986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197986).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resumes expired worktree scratch cleanup shortly after Gateway startup instead of waiting another full hour after every restart.
  [#197898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197898).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reclaims older expired scratch directories before scanning newly created worktrees on the first cleanup pass after a restart.
  [#197941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197941).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).



##### Update, packaging, and database maintenance updates

  - Releases unselected speculative archive payloads earlier during backup verification to reduce temporary disk usage.
    [#191107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191107).

    Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Stops read-only SQLite inspection snapshots from reflinking live database files, preventing database writes from stalling behind inspection copies.
  [#188009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188009).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reduces database statements and PostgreSQL advisory lock contention when committing session transcript turns.
  [#188354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188354).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Decompresses mixed SQLite and PostgreSQL backup archives only once during backup verification instead of making multiple full passes.
  [#188518](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188518).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refuses database ports early when required plugin migration contracts fail to load.
  [`cd3edd04cc`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/cd3edd04cce4363370f1ab2889d6128237d60be8).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Speeds up schema 39 upgrades on large agent databases by widening the transcript payload constraint in place instead of rebuilding the full transcript table.
  [#189224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189224).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes PostgreSQL native agent database handles failing to release their lease during close.
  [#189952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189952).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows offline dependency recovery to restore retained workspace owners without waking workers or reviving stale execution authority.
  [#190842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190842).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Labels slow Knowledge SQLite transaction warnings with the owning operation instead of reporting them as unlabeled.
  [#189534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189534).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Adds the blocking operation, thread ID, and hold age to slow SQLite and device-token lock wait diagnostics.
  [#189705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189705).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stops size-budget checks from failing local and dev-channel Git update builds.
  [#199002](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199002).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves the original Git runtime-staging failure when cleanup of the failed staging directory also errors.
  [#190928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190928).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Preserves Git metadata on failed update preflight worktrees when cleanup is refused so recovery remains possible.
  [#198217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198217).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Enables idle SQLite, session, and task workers to reclaim released memory on stock Bun.
  [#195229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195229).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates the managed Crabbox installer to require the published 0.82.0 release with runner-registration cancellation and portable cleanup fixes.
  [#194681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194681).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reports unpublished npm versions passed to openclaw update --tag as missing target versions instead of registry connectivity failures.
  [#193037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193037).

  Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Avoids repeated whole-database copies during offline update checks and migrations on large state databases.
  [#202985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202985).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces installed package file count to stay safely under the entry cap enforced by shipped updaters.
  [#190880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190880).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Bundles highlight.js as a single chunk to remove over 1,500 redundant files and keep installs under updater entry limits.
  [#191166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191166).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw update breaking a pnpm-linked Git checkout installation when switching to the published npm package.
  [#201701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201701).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Isolates unreadable or malformed retained npm install markers during update cleanup so affected files stay intact and other cleanup continues.
  [`3ff3932ac2`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3ff3932ac2527f6b5aadca0ef024bc09979ac8e2).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa) and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keeps retired plugin generations referenced by explicit executable pins during post-update cleanup so configured executables continue launching.
  [#191693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191693).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes scheduled Git backups failing on PostgreSQL-backed installations by using the online read-only per-store backup path.
  [#201870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201870).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Adds openclaw database prepare-state to prepare a specific PostgreSQL shared-state namespace during offline recovery.
  [#190832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190832).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Recovers state-lease heartbeats automatically after a dropped PostgreSQL connection instead of halting lease renewal.
  [#201858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201858).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Batches independent session watcher cursor updates on PostgreSQL to reduce write lock contention on remote databases.
  [#202092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202092).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Preserves prepared copy-on-write pnpm dependency trees in managed Docker and Podman reviews instead of reinstalling into a new store.
  [#195607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195607).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps sandboxed command HOME at the workspace root across task directories so repeated pnpm installs reuse one package store.
  [#195089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195089).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opens archived conversation history one bounded page at a time without waiting for the entire transcript archive to restore first.
  [#188397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188397).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes the manual source-checkout update documentation so its commands move a Git installation to the new upstream code instead of leaving it on old source.
  [#197371](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197371).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Emits a warning on Linux when a SQLite reader worker performs a heavy operation reading 64 MiB or more from disk.
  [#190445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190445).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tracks cumulative SQLite reader operations and bytes below the 64 MiB heavy-read threshold in memory diagnostics and Prometheus metrics.
  [#190907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190907).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Classifies SQLite storage worker out-of-memory failures and records actionable recovery guidance instead of a generic run error.
  [#192839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192839).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes supervised PostgreSQL updates exporting local database dumps and orphaning server-side captures when server recovery captures are configured.
  [#198469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198469).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Displays the full refusal reason in openclaw update repair output and failure reports when a transcript archive cannot be migrated.
  [#192918](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192918).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Rejects mixed-case `main` package targets such as `MAIN` or `openclaw@MAIN` before staging and directs users to `openclaw update --channel dev`.
  [#191994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191994).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Cleans up temporary npm git-source packing directories when an update step throws an error.
  [#190575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190575).
- Preserves JSON error output when openclaw update --json wizard fails unexpectedly.
  [#190946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190946).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Marks the hidden update migration-plan command as diagnostic only so its output is not mistaken for an authorized repair.
  [`f6ad30f473`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f6ad30f4732c43345cc21cbc627c06e50337625a).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Restores post-core plugin convergence when updating from 2026.6.x installations that do not pass an update run ID.
  [#190857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190857).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Distinguishes metadata, content, and executable-permission check failures in package content-inventory update reports.
  [#190625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190625).

  Thanks [@HermanZeng](https://github.com/HermanZeng).
- Fixes Git checkout update failure reports flagging unset Git tracking keys as failed checks or misreporting stale runtime artifacts as changed.
  [#200153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200153).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves SQLite contention errors and leading stack frames in saved update failure reports so long logs do not hide the cause.
  [#200322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200322).
- Stops clean openclaw update runs from printing a false warning about skipped agent database path repair.
  [#203023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203023).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes the update dialog claiming installation has started before progress arrives and shows "Reconnecting..." instead of troubleshooting steps during temporary disconnects.
  [#199254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199254).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Retains bounded local error details for package-update failures, avoids misleading install-root permission advice for outside paths, and keeps private exception prose out of public reports.
  [#194160](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194160).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Names the specific candidate check that failed when an update is refused with target-native-unsupported.
  [#175683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175683).

  Thanks [@yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee).
- Finds installed coding-agent CLIs on the invoking operator's PATH when launching Diagnose update failure.
  [#194058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194058).

  Thanks [@TheRealUnknownSoldier](https://github.com/TheRealUnknownSoldier).
- Checks host prerequisites such as the required Node version before starting an update and reports a single clear failure reason per attempt.
  [#198355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198355).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes OpenClaw failing to start if the first release-layout conversion update is abruptly killed partway through.
  [#200273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200273).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Stops plain openclaw update from silently settling ambiguous killed-update states and directs operators to the explicit update repair path.
  [#201361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201361).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Deletes pre-migration database snapshots on Git installs once an update activates cleanly, matching package installs and preventing repeated multi-gigabyte disk growth.
  [#191196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191196).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Prevents live update recovery checks from failing with `wal-copy-unstable` by reading metadata from live or cleanly closed SQLite databases without copying the full database.
  [#191332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191332).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes intermittent update rollbacks when upgrading from 2026.9.2 by trusting the published SQLite schema header over stale deferred admission state.
  [#191357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191357).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shows the underlying first failure behind an unfinished-recovery update refusal and notes when openclaw update repair can resolve it.
  [#192919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192919).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents an update from failing when a temporary SQLite snapshot copy cannot be deleted after a successful state read.
  [#193702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193702).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Preserves later operator stops during interrupted update recovery, leaves unowned services stopped with manual-start guidance, and retains terminal failure records.
  [#193885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193885).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes update rehearsal failing on healthy installations when an exec SecretRef depends on credentials kept outside scratch state.
  [#192261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192261).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skips unresolvable plugin links that traverse a regular file instead of aborting the entire openclaw update plugin-tree capture.
  [#191056](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191056).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves cancellation, rollback, and terminal failure reporting when a mutable update is interrupted after ledger writes are deferred.
  [#187998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187998).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Names the blocking `.openclaw-restore-*` directory and prints concrete recovery steps when an interrupted state restore blocks update commands.
  [#188304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188304).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves real Git clone and network error lines in update failure reports instead of collapsing them into a redacted diagnostic.
  [#188322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188322).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows `openclaw update` on an already-current npm installation to repair supported legacy configuration settings without requiring `--channel`.
  [#188716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188716).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes `openclaw update --channel dev` hanging on shallow clones with disconnected local `main` history and preserves extra local commits under `refs/openclaw/update-preserved/`.
  [#188892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188892).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restores the compatible npm plugin cohort and plugin settings when a core update rolls back after installing plugins that require the newer core.
  [#188978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188978).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes openclaw update repair refusing indefinitely when a verified update candidate failed a later step, allowing repair to settle forward.
  [#193132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193132).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Allows openclaw update repair to finish rolling back plugins when a plugin migration record or install entry changed after the update, keeping the newer change with a warning.
  [#193632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193632).

#### Messaging

You can now fork threaded conversations into independent siblings across Telegram, Discord, and Slack, link your own Discord account from your personal profile, and rely on steadier progress cards, formatting, and durable delivery across channels.


##### Fork threaded conversations in Telegram, Discord, and Slack


You can branch an existing channel thread into an independent sibling conversation with `/fork [title]`. OpenClaw copies the current conversation snapshot into a new ordinary session, keeps the original thread on its existing session, and posts a short literal recap of up to three recent visible text messages in the new thread with mentions and link previews disabled. Hidden reasoning and tool output are never posted, and a busy thread forks through its last completed turn rather than a partial answer.

Each platform keeps its own native boundaries. Telegram creates a sibling forum topic in the same supergroup when thread bindings are enabled and the bot has Manage Topics. Discord creates a sibling public thread, private thread, or forum post under the same parent channel, keeping private threads non-invitable and adding only the requester. Slack creates a new top-level root in the same channel when you send `@OpenClaw /fork [title]` as an ordinary message inside an existing channel thread. Direct messages, unthreaded channels, and Enterprise Grid org installs are not supported, and OpenClaw never retries creation automatically after an uncertain outcome so you can inspect the parent channel before trying again.
Control UI **Fork conversation** shares the same session-copy operation without inserting a duplicate recap. Forking does not clone the workspace or undo file or external effects.

[#190783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190783), [#190785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190785), [#190786](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190786).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).




##### Link your Discord account from your personal profile


Signed-in members can link their own Discord account from their personal profile page without asking an administrator to attest the link. Once verified through the browser-bound flow, requests you send from Discord retain your existing session and operator permissions without granting command ownership.
[#188910](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188910).

Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), and [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).




##### Telegram progress, topics, formatting, and delivery


- Makes Telegram progress cards easier to scan by using the tool-log icon row in quiet progress mode, hiding duplicate catalog-dispatcher rows, and showing readable operation names and context instead of internal identifiers.
  [#195225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195225).

- Fixes private DM topic routing and automatic naming when a client creates the topic before sending the first message, when the first message is a slash command or captionless media, or when an earlier auto-label attempt failed. Bound private topics also stay on their ACP session, keep their linked requester identity, and stop mixing cached context or cron notices across topics.
  [#169444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169444).

  Thanks [@aspalagin](https://github.com/aspalagin).

  [#184821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184821).

##### Discord access, voice transcription, and delivery


- Revokes access immediately when a Discord DM sender is removed from the allowlist or unpaired, even if an earlier session binding still exists for that conversation.
  [#196848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196848).

  Thanks [@drobison00](https://github.com/drobison00).
- Improves Discord voice meeting transcription by using live configuration and `gpt-transcribe` to filter non-speech noise, and releases partial voice recordings when speaking-end events are lost.
  [#202362](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202362).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  [#192932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192932).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Serves live Discord mentions ahead of recovery backlog after Gateway restarts, keeps slash-command reply text when an attachment fails to load, and renders edited Discord messages with the same Markdown tables, bold normalization, and mention aliases as initial sends.
  [#189420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189420).

  [#197825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197825).

  Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).

  [#199958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199958).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes lost replies to session events caused by internal UUID reply references, eliminates false failure notices on queued thread answers, preserves compaction notices in progress mode, finishes slash-command cleanup when obsolete commands are already gone, and settles queued REST requests when a caller aborts.
  [#196963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196963).

  Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).

  [#190717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190717), [#190722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190722).

  [#194150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194150).

  [#187555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187555).

  Thanks [@yy19971996](https://github.com/yy19971996).




##### Slack and Mattermost threads, progress, and formatting


- Keeps Slack progress editable through ordinary draft posts when native streaming expires, keeps expired progress behind the final answer, and settles progress cards cleanly after media replies.

- Renders agent-authored callback buttons on Mattermost and ensures taps arrive once, splits long Mattermost replies and media captions without breaking fenced code blocks, stops dropping person-authored posts that carry server link-preview cards, and prevents duplicate final answers when a post lands below a streaming preview.

##### Shared channel delivery, queues, commands, and audio


- Makes incoming audio recordings playable inline even when the sender did not set a voice-note flag, without eagerly downloading audio when you open a conversation.
  [#196787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196787).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Stops copying already-replayed session turns into supplemental channel history across WhatsApp, Signal, iMessage, Matrix, Microsoft Teams, Feishu, Slack, and Discord, and stops a steered follow-up answered with `NO_REPLY` from dropping an earlier visible answer.
  [#202637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202637).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

  [#196769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196769).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reauthorizes queued channel input before consuming it, keeps queued messages recoverable across plugin reloads and initial failures, delivers queued replies after the model turn closes, and records refused queued requests for the session owner without posting a channel error.
  [6858a6c9e3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6858a6c9e3f4e63d06e59915f1b65c0f918746e8).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Prevents progress cards from posting below a `message`-tool reply, stops duplicate progress drafts when a stale draft fails, and keeps consecutive tool calls grouped in one progress row across model waits.
