##### Additional messaging and channel updates


- Replaces generic runtime authority errors with clear messages explaining when a message turn ended or channel access was revoked.
  [`4eb5484161`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4eb54841619506e09f436cda9d72bff6d9894815).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes background job and session-event replies being silently dropped when a secrets or configuration reload occurs while the turn is running.
  [#193130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193130).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Preserves agent callback ownership across messaging adapters so interactive buttons continue routing to the owning agent even after a Gateway restart.
  [`bb5b0245bd`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bb5b0245bd43cbd83d20c2fe25fa84dd65c42668).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Shows actionable validation messages on stderr when openclaw channels resolve fails in JSON mode instead of printing a generic Doctor hint.
  [#199608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199608).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Shows actionable error messages on stderr when openclaw directory fails in JSON mode for blank group IDs or unsupported lookups.
  [#199616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199616).

  Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
- Shows actionable selector error messages on stderr when channel dead-letter commands fail in JSON mode with a blank channel or account.
  [#199658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199658).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Fixes blank `--account` and `--channel` errors on channel, directory, and message CLI commands being hidden behind a generic CLI failure message.
  [#198965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198965).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Reports required plugin installation failures clearly when running non-interactive openclaw channels add.
  [`a3e160cf68`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a3e160cf680cf33037401868970a6e0e081847e2).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes Buzz room lookup, WhatsApp live group lookup, and Microsoft Teams message search missing results when queries and stored text use different canonical Unicode forms.
  [#187953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187953).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Fixes Microsoft Teams live directory search missing channels when the query and stored display name use different canonical Unicode encodings.
  [#188003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188003).

  Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Fixes false identity errors when an authenticated channel user without a linked profile creates a session.
  [`5a20d6cf59`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a20d6cf596175597e68d4faf7a8bde6744cb225).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes openclaw channels list hanging for five seconds and printing fallback output after you press Ctrl+C.
  [#197815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197815).

  Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Fixes openclaw channels remove ignoring Ctrl+C while stopping the running channel and still deleting the account configuration.
  [#198019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198019).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Reports an unknown channel ID immediately in openclaw channels remove instead of prompting to disable a nonexistent channel first.
  [#195455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195455).

  Thanks [@yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee).
- Allows authorized users moving between the Web UI and a linked messaging channel in the same session to steer active turns without being treated as a different person.
  [#188029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188029).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Fixes missing private checkmark feedback in Discord when a matched plugin acknowledges a button interaction and then declines it.
  [#190083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190083).
- Re-checks sender access when a queued Discord message starts its turn so a sender removed while a previous turn was busy is not admitted.
  [#202330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202330).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Anchors Discord's 30-second REST entity cache to elapsed time so wall-clock corrections do not cause stale reads or extra requests.
  [#190997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190997).

  Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Routes background command results and approval follow-ups back into Discord auto-threads instead of the parent channel.
  [#190577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190577).
- Prevents duplicate CLI replies after an agent has already sent a final message for the current turn.
  [#194154](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194154).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Fixes invalid Feishu document styles when color markup uses JavaScript prototype property names such as constructor.
  [#189545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189545).
- Uses elapsed monotonic time for Feishu bot and sender name caches so host clock adjustments do not cause stale or premature refetches.
  [#192312](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192312).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Rejects internal background task UUIDs as Feishu reply anchors so completion replies stay in the right thread instead of failing with HTTP 400.
  [#177297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177297).

  Thanks [@can2049](https://github.com/can2049).
- Fixes Google Chat replies displaying self-labeled URLs and email addresses twice when falling back to plain text.
  [#197043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197043).

  Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Allows agents in message_tool mode to stay silent on optional bot messages in group chats when they have nothing useful to add.
  [#190467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190467).
- Keeps emoji whole instead of splitting them when truncating long participant display names in group threads and digests.

#### Memory

Knowledge gains record-derived entity Timelines, an opt-in decision-model recall gate with a bundled Rerank provider, self-updating scribes, and much faster vault writes, alongside a redesigned Memory UI and disk-reclaiming storage fixes.


##### Derived Knowledge timelines and leaner agent curation


Knowledge entity pages now derive their Timeline directly from dated source records instead of storing an ever-growing agent-written Timeline section in page prose. Frequently linked people and project pages no longer hit their page size budget from Timeline growth, while past-dated claims and retired Timeline entries stay preserved.

Knowledge tool results also drop repeated metadata, paths, and verbose connection summaries so agents spend far less context on every read, and write operations use hash-prefix fences to catch stale edits. Task-routed Knowledge skills leave already-current subject pages alone, fold dated material into page State without breaking citations, and show exact `knowledge_remember`, `knowledge_lookup`, and `knowledge_grep` call shapes up front so agents stop failing their first calls.
[#187676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187676), [#196767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196767), [#197018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197018), [#200339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200339).

Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).




##### Opt-in Knowledge recall gate with reranker support


Operators can opt into a per-turn, model-scored recall gate under `plugins.entries.knowledge.config.recallGate` that scores shortlisted Knowledge pages through the agent's configured Decision model before the main model answers. A new bundled Rerank provider connects to any `/rerank` endpoint such as vLLM, Hugging Face TEI, Infinity, llama.cpp, Cohere, Jina, or Voyage.

The gate is off until configured and starts in `shadow` mode for logging and threshold tuning before you switch to `live` injection. Both shadow and live modes send the user message and candidate excerpts to the configured Decision provider, and live mode injects model-selected page text rather than owner-approved summaries, so use a provider you trust with private vault content.
Existing owner-approved `knowledge_recall` summaries and the standing index remain unchanged. Unavailability, errors, or timeouts skip the optional injection.

[#188057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188057).

Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).




##### Knowledge scribes, Granola ingestion, and vault performance


- Keeps ongoing Knowledge scribes current after OpenClaw updates by running each scheduled pass in a fresh session of its conversation, updating unchanged run messages automatically while preserving your schedule and overrides, and offering **Replace with new…** when a customized message still contains retired wording. Retrying an update or replacement after a dropped connection no longer triggers duplicate turns.
  [#197391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197391), [#198030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198030).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  [#198783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198783).

  Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Fixes Knowledge Consolidation and Learn from Granola scribes stopping after a single item or deferring a Granola meeting when context looks short, and keeps the scribe from stalling at claim linking.
  [#197890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197890), [#199744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199744).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  [3f8da5e20c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f8da5e20cee3d7b01a29ff4d793aa5936f90825).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Cuts single-claim Knowledge save times on multi-thousand-record vaults from over ten seconds to roughly two seconds, keeps filtered searches and large vault rescans from blocking SQLite writers, reduces repeated scan work, and prevents large vaults from missing the project-memory recall budget.
  [#196294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196294).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

##### Memory storage, plugins, and shared workspaces


- Lets operators configure `agents.defaults.workspaceFileMode` (with per-agent overrides) so newly generated `MEMORY.md`, `DREAMS.md`, and dreaming files can be read by shared groups and inherited ACLs instead of defaulting to `0600`.
  [#188763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188763).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Returns disk space immediately after finished memory reindexes instead of holding deleted shadow files until Gateway restart, drops duplicate vectors from the embedding cache once the published index serves them, and reclaims archived legacy reindex shadows.

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).

#### Skills

Skill Workshop now ships as a bundled plugin, Templates can be created and reviewed inline from chat using a single `CLAW.md` manifest, Chat Details shows which skills were loaded, and skill discovery, settings, and packaging pick up broad reliability fixes.


##### Skill Workshop plugin and personal skill library


Skill Workshop now runs as a bundled `skill-workshop` plugin with its configuration under `plugins.entries.skill-workshop.config`, while personal skill-library authoring gets its own core `skill_library` tool. If the Skill Workshop plugin is turned off, its Control UI page now offers a working **Turn on Skill Workshop** action instead of failing with an unknown-method error, and hides the button when plugins are globally disabled. Background Skill Workshop reviews and Advisor runs also continue working right after the agent sends a reply to a channel.
[#189388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189388), [#190365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190365), [#192471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192471), [#191623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191623), [#192766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192766), [#192931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192931), [#193129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193129).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[#202308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202308).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).




##### Create and review Templates from chat with CLAW.md


You can ask an agent to turn its behavior into a reusable Template directly in an ordinary chat. Templates now configure native agent settings and exact ClawHub dependencies in one `CLAW.md` manifest, bundle complete skill folders (including executable scripts) with native trust checks, and open an inline read-only package review in the conversation before publishing.
[#195730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195730), [#196322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196322), [#196348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196348), [#196427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196427).

Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

[#198920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198920).

Thanks [@hehuang139](https://github.com/hehuang139).

[#196218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196218).

Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).

  Thanks [@wr-web](https://github.com/wr-web).
- Fixes standalone skill loading treating ignore patterns that start with whitespace before a hash as comments instead of excluding matching paths.
  [#192738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192738).

  Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Explains that a target session does not exist yet when attaching a skill library bundle before the session's first message instead of showing a generic error.
  [#201669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201669).

  Thanks [@freeqaz](https://github.com/freeqaz).
- Clarifies skill library selection, filtered empty states, and Skill Workshop editing guidance in Control UI.
  [#192282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192282).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Cleans up staged skill files when publishing a new skill fails due to a duplicate name conflict.
  [`7caf0c46d7`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7caf0c46d766e1f67566a9fd2227cb58fd34a5b2).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Honors nested ignore rules and keeps sibling skills available when a skill directory name contains bracket characters such as a[1].
  [#190973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190973).
- Reuses published skill search indexes across repeated searches instead of rebuilding unchanged indexes on the Gateway thread.
  [#195232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195232).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces memory held by live skill search catalogs across concurrent sessions while keeping search results and permissions unchanged.
  [#195418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195418).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses skill-search indexes across sessions and worktrees when only companion files change and skill instructions stay identical.
  [#196147](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196147).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shares identical skill catalog prompts and compact metadata across fresh sessions with the same eligible skills to reduce retained memory.
  [#197012](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197012).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses tokenized skill search bodies across repeated searches to reduce latency and memory churn on large skill catalogs.
  [#193549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193549).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes automatic skill watching turning back on when an in-flight status read or watcher cleanup finishes after watching was disabled.
  [#193399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193399).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Adds the bundled Skill Workshop icon and activity artwork instead of showing fallback initials.
  [#190212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190212).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Bounds transcript lookups in Skill Workshop so reviewing large tool-heavy sessions does not exhaust transcript workers.
  [#193544](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193544).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes the Agent Skills panel showing an empty-skills state while the initial skills list is still loading or after a failed read.
  [#189191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189191).

  Thanks [@huwei-xydt](https://github.com/huwei-xydt).
- Defers loading Skills detail, Markdown reader, and library dialogs until opened so the Skills page renders faster on high-latency connections.
  [#194827](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194827).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reads authorized skills from delivered turn bundles in worker environments.
  [`e73c851a69`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e73c851a69040295125f7e02415d9bd71aff8019).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes Auto Review calls hanging past their timeoutSeconds budget during Git capture or delivery by applying the timeout across the entire call and reporting the stalled phase.
  [#203433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203433).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).



#### Native Apps

The macOS app gains OpenClaw ID sign-in and device enrollment, macOS and iOS keep chat drafts across restarts, and iOS, Android, Wear OS, and desktop apps add Talk mute controls, sidebar group controls, watch assistant support, and ClawHub review links.



##### macOS app

  The macOS app now supports signing in with OpenClaw ID to connect as your primary Gateway route, approving your Mac as a device, enrolling Mac nodes through invitations with native hosting onboarding, or joining a claw as a member by invite or from another of your devices. An app-owned loopback proxy serves the embedded Dashboard over OpenClaw ID (OpenClaw ID remote access on the Gateway remains in **Settings -> Labs**).

Native chat on macOS and iOS now persists composer drafts and picked attachments across app restarts, and both macOS and iOS expose a direct navigation route to the **Systems** dashboard.
[#196759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196759), [#197585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197585), [#197630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197630), [#198499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198499).

Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

[#197846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197846).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[#164462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164462).

Thanks [@genaro23](https://github.com/genaro23).
- Offers OpenClaw ID recovery when a Mac is removed from a claw, and fixes onboarding **Set up later** stopping a Gateway installed outside the app.
  [#197977](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197977).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  [#192737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192737).

  Thanks [@sukhdeepjohar](https://github.com/sukhdeepjohar).

- Retains queued input across native reconnects, raises macOS exec-host request limits to 1 MiB, fixes empty output on invalid UTF-8, uses the recognition language for Talk pause timing, and cuts full Apple chat-history downloads across refreshes from 13 to 1.

##### iOS and iPadOS app

#### Models and Providers

This release expands model coverage across Google, OpenAI, MiniMax, Tencent, Baseten, Parallel, and ACPX while defaulting to the v3 model catalog and adding custom provider branding. It also enables native Claude Code web search by default, shifts ordinary Codex threads to OpenClaw's base prompt, adds per-model exec ceilings, and expands ClawRouter to native search and speech offers.


##### New models and provider capabilities


Google AI Studio gains catalog support for Gemini 4 Hydrogen (`google/gemini-4-hydrogen`) with a 1,048,576-token context window, 65,536 default output tokens, and low, medium, and high thinking levels, while Gemini 4 Carbon joins the recommended model list and new Gemini releases now sync automatically from models.dev. All Gemini text models on the official AI Studio endpoint now default to the Interactions API, and `/fast on` maps to `service_tier=priority` on that path (Gemma models and custom proxies stay on GenerateContent, and setting `api` to `google-generative-ai` keeps explicit `cachedContent` handles). Google also exposes an authority-bound managed-research adapter.
For Gemini 4 Hydrogen, `xhigh` currently sends `high` and context caching is implicit only. Explicit `serviceTier` or `service_tier` model parameters (`standard`, `flex`, or `priority`) override `/fast` when set.

[#197863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197863), [#197178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197178), [#189942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189942).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[#196988](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196988).

[#193341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193341).

Thanks [@markmcd](https://github.com/markmcd).

[#173966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173966).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

Image generation expands with OpenAI GPT Image 2.6 Sunburst (`openai/gpt-image-2.6-sunburst`) and Flare (`openai/gpt-image-2.6-flare`) through the direct Images API, alongside MiniMax-H3-Image and MiniMax-H3-Image-Highspeed on both `minimax` and `minimax-portal` with up to 16 reference images and `1K`, `2K`, or `4K` resolution control. Retired MiniMax M2 models are no longer offered, and MiniMax PDF extraction moves to `MiniMax-M3`.
OpenClaw does not yet send the GPT Image 2.6 `complexity` field, markup-guided edits, SVG output, or animated GIF output. GPT Image 2.5 and 2.6 subscription access is not established by API-key setup. MiniMax H3 requests default to a 610-second timeout when none is configured.

[#194723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194723).

Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).

[#196627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196627).

Thanks [@SaladDay](https://github.com/SaladDay).

[#203998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203998).

Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

Additional provider additions include Tencent Hunyuan Hy4 Flash (`tencent-tokenhub/hy4-flash` and `tencent-tokenplan/hy4-flash`) with a 1,048,576-token context window and vision support, Baseten's Base Labs joey-1 (`baseten/decision-models/baseten-joey-2b-latest`) for typed decisions, and Parallel Decisions Fast (`parallel/decisions-fast`, `openrouter/parallel/decisions-fast`, and `openrouter/parallel/decisions-fast:free`) for web-grounded typed decisions natively and through OpenRouter. Installed Grok Build (`acp-grok-build/<id>`) and Qoder CLI (`acp-qoder/<id>`) models can now be selected directly in the native chat picker through ACPX.
Baseten hosted Decision models enforce a 16,384-token context limit per question and reject oversized inputs. Parallel Decisions runs live web research on every request, so send only context that may be evaluated externally. Grok Build and Qoder own their own sign-in and billing on the Gateway host.

[#199957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199957).

##### Model catalog, picker, and custom providers


Custom model providers can now set display names and artwork through `models.providers.<id>.name` and `icon` (local files, public HTTPS URLs, or bundled icons) without changing provider IDs, routing, or existing sessions. The remote model catalog now defaults to v3, carrying typed Decision capabilities into the picker while tolerating newer publisher fields and keeping earlier downloaded catalogs serving offline until a v3 refresh completes.
[#193952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193952), [#189678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189678), [#189688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189688).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

[#190187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190187), [#197204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197204).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

Model picker visibility is now separated from per-model metadata. Entries under `agents.defaults.models` or per-agent `models` no longer pin models into the picker, and `openclaw configure` keeps provider models dynamic instead of creating restrictive allowlists. Confirmed non-chat and typed-only models are hidden from chat pickers while guessed non-chat IDs stay callable by exact reference, and `runtime` or `refreshable` providers never list models omitted by their live account listing.
[#190095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190095), [#190112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190112), [#199998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199998), [#200102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200102), [#197326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197326), [#202618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202618).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

##### Anthropic and Claude CLI


OpenClaw-managed `claude-cli` turns now keep Claude Code's native `WebSearch` enabled by default when no OpenClaw web search provider is configured or pinned, so Claude logins can search the web out of the box (configure `managedNativeTools` with an empty list to turn it off). On direct API-key requests to `api.anthropic.com`, `/fast` now enables native fast mode on Claude Fable 5.2 and future Claude models while falling back once to standard speed if Anthropic rejects the `speed` parameter. On `claude-cli`, OpenClaw now reads thinking-off and fast-mode support directly from Claude Code's model menu and passes `--fast-mode on` when supported.
Upgrading from a release before native `WebSearch` became default changes the launch tool list, so each existing `claude-cli` conversation starts one fresh Claude Code session on its next turn while preserving OpenClaw chat history.

[#197051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197051), [#197291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197291), [#196774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196774), [#197033](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197033).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

[#174333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174333).

Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).

  [#187117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187117).

##### Codex runtime and agent execution


Ordinary native Codex threads now receive OpenClaw's thread-stable policy sections as their base instructions instead of stacking OpenClaw guidance on top of Codex's built-in prompt, cutting repeated prompt overhead on fresh sessions. Enrolled cloud workers can now host the pinned Codex app-server directly through the opt-in `plugins.entries.codex.config.appServer.workerHostedCloud` setting using a worker-only private auth bundle.
Worker-hosted cloud inference requires a Full remote-exec placement and the versioned `codex.app-server.stdio.v1.<version>` node command; there is no local fallback when the worker cannot start.

[#191344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191344).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).

[#186646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186646), [#192539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192539).

Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).

Operators can now cap host exec permissions per provider or model with `tools.byProvider.<ref>.exec.mode`, letting a specific model run behind the exec reviewer (`auto`) or stricter approvals while other models keep their normal policy. The embedded agent loop also replaces its legacy multi-detector tool-loop heuristics with a single arguments-only repeat guard that adds a redirect note on the 3rd consecutive identical call and stops the turn with `tool_repeat_limit` on the 20th.
Repeatable polling and wait tools such as `agents_wait`, `sessions_yield`, `process` poll/log/list, and `computer` screenshot/wait declare themselves repeatable and skip the repeat guard.

[#193566](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193566).

Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

[#199591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199591).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

##### ClawRouter


ClawRouter now discovers and invokes declared native catalog offers for `web_search` and speech synthesis (both TTS and Talk), including catalog-scoped native Gradium speech via `upstreamProvider: "gradium"` without needing a separate Gradium key. Speech synthesis refreshes the selected key's catalog offers before running and fails closed on unknown or denied formats.
Native Gradium speech requires an active Worker implementation and an eligible offer in the selected proxy key's current catalog.

[#191792](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191792), [#191863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191863), [#192052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192052).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keeps shared ClawRouter keys off cooldown after an upstream credit failure and falls back immediately to the next configured model instead of retrying the exhausted route.
  [#192575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192575), [#193250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193250).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes GPT models routed through ClawRouter filling every optional tool argument with placeholders and prevents forced recommendation fields during document sends.
  [#196083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196083).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

  [#192764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192764).

  Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- Fixes uploaded image edits using the selected OpenAI model, uses runtime fetch for manifest embeddings, and keeps truncated web search results well-formed.
  [#197897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197897).

  Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).

  [99652357ad](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/99652357adba0bbfcdaa18277b7d26ea9d3dffce).

  [#195866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195866).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Honors selected endpoint model capabilities and credential-scoped wire formats across ClawRouter offers, discovering Fusion through unified offers and preserving native Mistral, Cohere, xAI Responses, and Vercel Messages transport and replay behavior.
  [#191762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191762), [#191765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191765), [#191776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191776), [#191771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191771), [#191810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191810), [#191829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191829), [#192060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192060).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#197615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197615).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).




##### Additional model and provider updates


- Carries bounded prior conversation context when switching an existing chat to the Agents API runtime and recovers recorded work when returning to a native session.
  [#196467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196467).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reports the full 500,000-token context window for Grok 4.6 and its inference profiles on Amazon Bedrock instead of the 32K fallback.
  [#192927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192927).

  Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Omits the unsupported temperature parameter on us.xai.grok-4.6 and global.xai.grok-4.6 Bedrock Converse requests to prevent HTTP 400 failures.
  [#192958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192958).

  Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Fixes newly released Claude models such as Fable 5.2 losing reasoning and returning HTTP 400 signature errors across tool loops when accessed through proxies or custom Anthropic base URLs.
  [#196459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196459).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reuses shared agent system instructions in the Anthropic prompt cache across new sessions for the same agent, reducing repeated cache writes.
  [#196471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196471).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@scoootscooob](https://github.com/scoootscooob).
- Fixes Claude subscription setup-token requests to newly released Claude models such as Fable 5.2 failing with a Claude Code version-floor error when the host CLI is unavailable.
  [#196693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196693).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Stops retrying Anthropic 429 responses caused by missing credits as transient rate limits and applies billing error handling instead.

  Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- Refreshes the reported Claude Code CLI version on the next Anthropic OAuth request after an in-place update without needing a Gateway restart.

#### Automations and Scheduling

Authenticated users can now manage their own conversation-owned automations with `operator.cron`, failing automations reach their owner conversation on the first failure, and scheduled condition checks stay responsive even when all eight run slots are busy. This release also fixes self-scratch access, image-generating cron runs, restart and worker recovery, and CLI validation.


##### Scoped automation access and ownership


The new `operator.cron` scope (included in `operator.write`) lets authenticated users create and manage their own conversation-owned automations through agent turns without requiring Gateway administrator access. Ownership and caller grants are preserved across handed-off dashboard chats, shared conversations, channel edits, and trusted-proxy setting updates.
Under trusted-proxy authentication, changing `trustedProxies`, `userHeader`, or `requiredHeaders` keeps existing jobs running, while removing the user from `allowUsers`, changing their identity scopes, or leaving trusted-proxy mode refuses them.

##### Failure repair, notifications, and plugin observations


Owned automations with inherited failure routes now notify their owner conversation from the first execution failure rather than waiting for a second failure, with a 1-hour cooldown for new causes and a daily reminder for the same unresolved error. Failed `cron_changed` plugin hooks can also inspect an optional, content-free `agentTerminal` snapshot describing the selected agent attempt without reading message text.
Command jobs, `on-exit` and stream schedules, and jobs without an owner conversation continue to send standard failure alerts. The auto-disable safety backstop remains 10 consecutive failures.

##### Condition checks, concurrency, and run reliability


Scheduled condition checks no longer consume one of the scheduler's eight active run slots, and checks that wait on the three concurrent evaluation slots now queue in due-time order instead of backing off. Conditional automations stay responsive even while eight long runs are active, and fired payloads wait for the next free slot without burning their execution timeout.
Checks started by manual or stream runs, and checks for script-payload jobs, continue to acquire a run slot first.

#### Browser and Computer Use

You can now take temporary control of the agent's browser from the Browser panel, open Chrome settings and password manager tabs privately out of the agent's reach, try the experimental Cloudflare Kitesurf engine, and rely on steadier managed browser sessions.


##### Take control of the agent's browser from the Browser panel


You can click **Take control** in the Control UI Browser panel toolbar to solve a CAPTCHA, finish a sign-in, or edit a form without the agent acting over you. While you hold control, browser actions from the agent's browser tool, the `openclaw browser` CLI, and other Control UI windows are refused, and the agent receives a normal result explaining that you have taken over so its turn can continue. The agent can still take snapshots and screenshots while you hold control, and an action that had already started may finish.

Click **Release control** to hand the browser back. Control also releases automatically when the Control UI window closes or disconnects, or after five minutes without deliberate input from you. **Take control** is available for browsers on the Gateway host, not for node-routed browsers, and ordinary clicks or typing without clicking **Take control** leave the agent's browser results unchanged. The Browser panel also cuts typing lag and screencast stutter so interactive sessions feel responsive.
[#197928](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197928), [#198454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198454), [#197927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197927).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).




##### Open Chrome settings and password manager privately in the Browser panel


You can now open `chrome://settings` (such as `chrome://settings/payments`) and `chrome://password-manager` from the Browser panel address bar to manage payment cards or saved passwords yourself. Unlike **Take control**, these pages are isolated from the agent completely. They always open in a new tab accessible only to your authenticated Control UI connection, and the agent's browser tool, the `openclaw browser` CLI, and other Gateway clients cannot open, list, read, capture, or interact with them even while you have one open. Other `chrome://` pages remain blocked for everyone.

This works for locally managed and CDP-attached Chromium profiles on the Gateway host. It is not available for node-routed browsers, Chrome MCP existing-session profiles, the browser extension, or headless-shell profiles. If Chrome asks for your device password before revealing a saved password, that prompt appears on the computer running the browser rather than inside the panel.
[#197904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197904).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).




##### Experimental Cloudflare Kitesurf browser engine


You can attach OpenClaw's browser tool to an externally managed [Cloudflare Kitesurf](https://github.com/cloudflare/kitesurf) CDP endpoint by configuring a profile with `engine: "kitesurf"` and `attachOnly: true`. The adapter supports navigation, tabs, text, ARIA snapshots, semantic clicks, typing, form filling, evaluation, and experimental rendered screenshots without running a Chromium process.

OpenClaw does not install, launch, or deploy Kitesurf, and targets last only for the active CDP connection, so do not use this profile to preserve logins. PDF export, downloads, uploads, dialogs, emulation, logs, visual actions, and action batches remain Chromium-only, and any deployed Kitesurf endpoint must be protected with your own authentication because upstream ships without built-in auth.
[#198833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198833).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

#### Plugins and Integrations

OpenClaw adds bundled Deep Research with a dedicated Control UI panel, graduates Advisor out of Labs, and expands Workboard, MCP, ACP, plugin suggestions, and PostgreSQL store support.



##### Deep Research

  Open **Explore panels** in chat and select **Deep research** to launch and monitor research jobs, or call the `deep_research` tool directly. Deep Research ships as a bundled, default-enabled plugin with lazy startup that does not resolve credentials or make network requests until you use it, and you can disable it with `plugins.entries.deep-research.enabled: false`.

The default `portable` engine runs bounded, evidence-backed research with your existing model, search, and fetch tools, supporting exact quotations, explicit gaps, domain allow and deny filters, and supplied local documents. For provider-managed runs, you can opt in to `google` managed research with a pinned Google API-key profile to use Google's Gemini Interactions API (`deep-research-preview-04-2026`), where API billing and provider storage apply. Jobs and operation receipts are retained for seven days with up to 32 jobs per conversation, support idempotent start, status, result, resume, and cancel actions, and automatically recover after Gateway restarts through durable wakes that revalidate current conversation authority.
[#173964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173964), [#173969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173969), [#173974](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173974), [#176887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176887), [#177230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/177230).

Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes bounded research text cutting astral characters such as emoji or CJK Extension B in half at UTF-16 length limits.
  [#195596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195596).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Explains clearly when Deep Research is loaded on a Gateway without session-work support instead of showing a misleading conversation-authority error, and restores bundled artwork and distinct chat panel icons for Deep research and Knowledge.
  [#193972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193972), [#197487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197487).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#200887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200887).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Fixes Deep Research and other plugin services failing to start after module reloads when shared-state admission retains an earlier module graph.
  [#194113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194113).



##### Workboard

  Boards can now run multiple active cards per owner at the same time by setting `orchestration.maxActivePerOwner` between 1 and 20 (default 1), so independent cards for one agent or the unassigned dispatcher no longer queue single-file. Workers can also record optional claims when completing a card, and checkers can record observations and verification verdicts without making evidence mandatory.
[#197471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197471), [#198358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198358).

Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).
- Fixes a retried card's earlier superseded run completing the card with a stale result after a retry has already started.
  [#198456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198456).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).
- Settles cards from owner lifecycle events instead of attempt-level `agent_end` hooks, recovers automatically after PostgreSQL connection loss, attributes slow card-list waits, and loads Workboard database port SQL from its API.
  [#192413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192413).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#192017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192017).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#188782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188782).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#191340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191340).



##### Advisor

  Advisor moves out of Labs into standard plugin settings while remaining off by default under Settings -> Plugins -> Advisor. Reviews default to every 20 minutes without repeating prior advice in the same run, now cover subagent runs alongside main-agent user runs, and log both cache writes and cancelled in-flight reviews.
[#196704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196704), [#197020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197020), [#197029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197029), [#197506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197506), [#199676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199676).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reports policy-blocked Advisor reviews cleanly as skipped with the policy reason instead of logging an unexpected fork failure.
  [0d9a4c5a12](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0d9a4c5a12f84ca833e6938a419a115b0b0b0b9f).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).



##### MCP servers, worker nodes, and Connected apps

  Admitted MCP tools can now run directly on the worker node that hosts their MCP client, and node-hosted MCP tools can ask you form or URL questions through the Gateway when `mcp.apps.enabled` is turned on. Configured MCP tools can also be invoked through `tools.invoke` and HTTP `/tools/invoke`, appear in the usage-ranked tool index, and be added or edited from the CLI (`openclaw mcp add`, `set`, `unset`, `configure`, and `tools`) without stopping a running Gateway.
[#191387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191387), [#191589](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191589).

Thanks [@stevenlee-oai](https://github.com/stevenlee-oai).

[#187699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187699), [#198348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198348).

[#200582](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200582).

Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

##### Plugin suggestions, permissions, and runtime

  Native plugins and Codex bundles can now declare up to three manifest-backed `recommendedPrompts` that appear on the plugin detail page without executing plugin code. Picking a prompt runs the existing install or enable flow and opens an editable chat draft prefilled with the plugin mention and prompt text. Plugin prompt-modification permissions are also explained more clearly in settings and renamed from `allowPromptInjection` to `allowPromptModification` with automatic Doctor config migration.
[#186593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186593), [#192365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192365), [#192634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192634).

##### PostgreSQL plugin and state stores

  All-PostgreSQL installations now hold Gateway state ownership directly in PostgreSQL instead of relying only on a local SQLite lock file, and `openclaw database ownership status` reports the recorded supervisor. PostgreSQL stores also reuse prepared statements, read each chat history page from one database snapshot, and support porting and backing up plugin blobs up to 128 MiB.
[#184013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184013), [#190001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190001), [#196237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196237).

Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).

Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Recovers Logbook and Team Reports automatically after a PostgreSQL connection loss, and fixes GitHub publication failing when session or shared state uses PostgreSQL.
  [#192011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192011), [#192020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192020).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#193749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193749).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Keeps PostgreSQL leases alive during lock contention, lets fresh PostgreSQL installs port back to SQLite, gives large maintenance operations a one-hour SQL budget, repairs PostgreSQL session keys with boards, and handles empty SQLite `AUTOINCREMENT` sequences cleanly during porting.
  [#201853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201853), [#201857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201857), [#201867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201867), [#201869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201869).

- Fixes restored PostgreSQL homes failing on first start, inline API-key failure recording on PostgreSQL agent stores, numeric scores in PostgreSQL session recall, and PostgreSQL search DDL dimension validation.
  [#189901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189901), [#190291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190291).

  [#202304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202304).

##### GitHub publication and bundled integrations

  `github_publish` can now edit verified pull requests and request ClawSweeper re-review by explicit PR URL without importing source or binding a workspace. Publishing the same local checkout again updates its existing PR instead of opening a new one, preserves the conversation workspace, lets maintainers prepare PRs in guest conversations and land PRs published by other sessions, and fixes project chats failing with an "already has another source" error.

##### MCP, ACP, and external integration updates

  - Fixes active ACP replies being cut off by the 6-minute reply watchdog while tools are still streaming results.

  Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).

  [#176886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/176886).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Skips unnecessary plugin setup captures during Gateway startup for already-enabled non-channel plugins.

- Reuses the matching prepared plugin registry during plugin-started subagent completions instead of rebuilding it unnecessarily.
  [#190781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190781).

- Fixes openclaw plugins update --all continuing to install remaining plugins and hook packs after you press Ctrl+C.

- Reports both the original installer failure and any lease-cleanup failure when a plugin update fails.

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Prevents plugin updates from attempting a failed rollback twice or rolling back a deferred install prematurely.
  [#191182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191182).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes openclaw plugins update aborting an entire update batch when one plugin ships no Doctor artifact and has a pending migration.

- Preserves typed npm and ClawHub installer failure codes in plugin update error summaries.

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Aligns task-authored plugin archive review with the standard 50,000-entry installer limit instead of rejecting valid archives above 512 entries.
  [#188866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188866).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Fixes cold Gateways backed by PostgreSQL failing to start when preparing valid plugin-backed secrets.

- Allows QuickJS code execution to recover automatically after a temporary engine initialization failure without restarting the Gateway.
  [#191952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191952).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Retains prepared plugin scopes and borrowed provider auth across worker startup instead of falling back to the bundled catalog.

- Lets agents review and install published ClawHub Templates through the setup helper without shell access while keeping exact-plan and dependency consent.

- Preserves password, data-parser, and child-interpreter APIs for downloaded plugins in trimmed standalone AOT builds.

- Fixes standalone builds failing to load the embedded Acorn parser for fetched Teams and TypeScript plugins when static module paths are minified as template strings.

- Fixes `openclaw templates build` rejecting a package-root `BOOTSTRAP.md` file between 1 MiB and 2 MiB that already passed `templates validate`.

- Fixes Template agent avatars failing to resolve when the declared avatar path differs from the workspace file path only by backslashes or letter case.

- Fixes previewing files of a creator Template via a `@publisher/slug` link failing when the Template is stored under an unscoped name.

- Accepts publisher-qualified `@publisher/slug` references in `openclaw templates add` for creator-published Templates stored under unscoped package names.
  [#198479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198479).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Fixes a failed `openclaw templates add` preview against a Gateway warning that the add may be partial even though previews make no changes.

- Corrects the Templates CLI documentation so authors know `CLAW.md` must live at the package root for `validate`, `dev`, and `build`.
  [#198876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198876).

  Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b).
- Stops Template greetings on CLI backends like Claude CLI from logging false warnings about unavailable core tools in `tools.allow`.

- Displays disabled automation declarations as "Disabled" instead of "Paused" in Template details, previews, and draft reviews.

- Attaches the new chat immediately during Template Add while dependencies finish installing, preserving queued messages and allowing failed openings to retry after reconnect.

- Restores default workspace files like `AGENTS.md` when an offline `openclaw templates add` keeps the newly created agent after a later step fails.
  [#199082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199082).

  Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b).
- Fixes enabled tool-output reducers such as Tokenjuice being silently skipped in headless runs and nested Code Mode tool calls.
  [#199434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199434).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Preserves Twilio conversation greetings without media streams and keeps calls listening for up to 120 seconds of silence instead of disconnecting after five seconds.
  [#193137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193137).
- Applies Visitor Access limit changes (`maxVisitors` and `defaultTtlDays`) in place without queuing a full plugin reload behind active agent runs.
  [#191477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191477).

- Includes Twilio's numeric error code and documentation link when a voice call is refused.

- Adds an opt-in Twilio configuration option in the Voice Call plugin to record outbound calls.

  Thanks [@DCastile](https://github.com/DCastile).
- Improves outbound Voice Call errands by guiding agents to place the ongoing task in the call brief instead of only in the spoken opening.
  [#190110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190110).
- Fixes realtime Voice Call transcripts dropping spaces or characters after overlapping Unicode words whose lowercase form expands.

  Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559).
- Forwards the configured brief language to realtime input transcription on outbound voice calls.
  [#190703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190703).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes plugins declaring openclaw as a peer dependency failing to load on Windows when the OpenClaw install folder uses different letter casing.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete) and [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).

  [#199387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199387).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Fixes Workboard displaying disabled linked automations as "Paused" instead of "Disabled" in the board header, tooltip, and card details.
  [#199288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199288).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Stops Workboard from claiming no cards match and showing zero filter counts while its initial card read is pending or has failed.
  [#199370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199370).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Stops Workboard Sessions boards from showing zero counts and empty columns before initial reads or people-filter changes finish.
  [#199375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199375).
