- Stops duplicate floating error toasts from covering the Save button after a failed save in Workboard's Sessions-board editor.
  [#199380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199380).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Shows the first worker-start failure reason alongside the total failure count in Workboard Start agents receipts and describes triage cards awaiting planning accurately.
  [#199334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199334).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Preserves authoritative board totals in Workboard while cards are paginated instead of shrinking counts to the loaded page.
  [#181164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/181164).

  Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour).
- Fixes new work sessions started from X failing before their first reply when the spawning turn finishes during workspace preparation.
  [#191760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191760).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Caps X API and media-upload responses at 16 MiB to prevent unbounded Gateway memory growth.
  [#190526](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190526).

  Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen).
- Applies backpressure to the X Activity stream reader so slow mention processing does not cause unbounded Gateway memory growth.
  [#191161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191161).

  Thanks [@sogav21](https://github.com/sogav21).
- Reduces temporary disk writes during GitHub publication base refreshes by materializing only changed files instead of rebuilding full temporary checkouts.
  [#200748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200748).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes plugins being able to override host-owned reply expectations and silence required replies.
  [`586df95eab`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/586df95eabb3bd68ed7adb5f155beb4afd9ad478).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).




#### Security and Privacy

This release adds certificate and redirect remediations, tightens OpenClaw ID access and revocation checks, and improves credential and redaction hygiene across Gateway and tool paths. It also keeps queued follow-up actions, node requests, and read-only diagnostics within current permissions.


##### Certificates, redirects, and egress controls


- Updates the bundled LanceDB native dependency to `0.41.0` so compromised certificate chains are rejected.
  [#188801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188801).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Confines redirects on configured self-hosted web-tool endpoints to their configured host so private-network fetches cannot redirect requests to other hosts.
  [#196847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196847).

  Thanks [@drobison00](https://github.com/drobison00).
- Blocks untrusted workspace `.env` files from setting the Azure OpenAI resource variable and redirecting credentialed model requests to another host.
  [#202031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202031).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).
- Warns at Gateway startup when the running Node build lacks the recommended certificate security patches, and honors TLS when probing configured SSH endpoints.
  [#193710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193710).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#190102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190102).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
- Launches durable background `exec` commands without short-lived secret-egress proxy grants, sentinels, or proxy settings because those grants end when the Gateway stops.
  Durable commands use standard environment configuration instead of ephemeral egress proxy grants.

  [#188674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188674).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes the secret egress proxy returning `502 Bad Gateway` for allowed hostnames longer than the certificate Common Name limit, and allows stock Node 24 `fetch` inside `exec` to reach local HTTP servers when the egress proxy is active.
  [#153661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153661).

  Thanks [@alter](https://github.com/alter).

  [#186765](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186765).

  Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin).




##### OpenClaw ID, node, and connected-app access enforcement


- Closes OpenClaw ID revocation and freshness gaps so delegated owner devices cannot escape revocation or regain removed capabilities, replayed revocations cannot remove a re-approved device, clients follow a re-bind, and a stolen Gateway key cannot retire bindings.
  [#193990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193990), [#193146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193146), [#196402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196402).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Expires bound nodes together with their approving owner device certificate, discards pending foreground node actions after OpenClaw ID access is removed, and drops queued node requests or voice transcripts after a node is unpaired.
  [#198553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198553), [#198412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198412), [#198684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198684).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Rechecks queued desktop launch requests before starting apps so access changes take effect while worker reconciliation waits, and keeps desktop screen and audio streams bound to their requesting OpenClaw ID device.
  [#198013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198013), [#198063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198063).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Keeps OpenClaw ID member roles authoritative across mentions, HTTP session history, media download tickets, connected-app HTTP panels, new session creation, and message action sends even when a linked local profile has broader permissions.
  [#199919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199919), [#199946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199946), [#200007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200007), [#200070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200070), [#199413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199413), [#199863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199863).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Ties connected apps to their approving OpenClaw ID membership, preserves member ownership on token-authenticated Gateways without local roles, and stops Connected apps from suggesting pairing when the current role lacks permission.
  [#196276](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196276), [#196496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196496).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  [#199248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199248).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).




##### Runtime policies, sandboxing, and queued follow-up rechecks


- Preserves visitor access-policy checks after a turn ends, including queued follow-ups and failed-before-reply settlement, while keeping plugin retirement and quiescence fail-closed.
  [#198844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198844).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents node tools that claim an unverified plugin owner from passing plugin-ID tool policies.
  [#196907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196907).

  Thanks [@drobison00](https://github.com/drobison00).
- Restricts shared Actions widgets to approved named queries, honors Gateway embed policies in Canvas document clients, and preserves authored self-only CSP headers in relative HTML previews.
  [#186057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186057).

  Thanks [@TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  [#180520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180520).

  Thanks [@bkudiess](https://github.com/bkudiess).

  [#188013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188013).

  Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Isolates agent `exec` disk I/O in delegated Linux cgroups when configured and disables core dumps for Linux agent commands so crashes do not spill process memory or stall host storage.
  [#188379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188379), [#196772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196772).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Escapes Markdown link delimiters in extracted web-fetch anchors and reports a security error instead of crashing with a `TypeError` when a CLI `--url` value is malformed.
  [#199292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199292).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).

  [#198728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198728).

  Thanks [@YangManBOBO](https://github.com/YangManBOBO).




##### Credential hygiene and read-only disclosure limits


- Stops Gog's Gmail watcher from exposing the Gmail push token and shared OpenClaw hook token in local process arguments, and keeps Firecrawl search and scrape API keys confined to their respective endpoints.
  [#192019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192019).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).

  [#190706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190706).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).
- Preserves multiline secret redaction in captured OpenTelemetry content and refuses redaction sentinel markers inside JSONC `+key` names in `oc-path`.
  [#194353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194353).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).

  [#193258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193258).

  Thanks [@KhanCold](https://github.com/KhanCold).
- Hides host filesystem paths from read-only `system.info` responses and omits the host Node executable path from read-only Gateway health and status responses.
  [#191897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191897).

  Thanks [@pgondhi987](https://github.com/pgondhi987).

  [#197022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197022).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).
- Refuses writerless FIFOs instead of hanging when reading native-hook credentials, recovery manifests, image-edit inputs, or daemon diagnostic logs, ignores broken external credential symlinks during discovery, and names the state directory when it cannot be made private.
  [#191423](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191423), [#191347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191347).

  Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).

  [#193987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193987).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  [#172294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172294).

  Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).

  [#190879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190879).

  Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).

  [`72b6667654`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/72b6667654d0b172d5506bb2ad450f0d8f9fb9ca).

  Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b), [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).




##### Additional security and privacy updates


- Allows authenticated administrators with a named operator role to use eligible Access programs even when the role does not list accessPrograms explicitly.
  [#194341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194341).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Prevents standing-grant approval cards and grant-list previews from splitting emoji across truncation limits.
  [#167259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167259).

  Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen).
- Checks delegated system-agent approval authority once at request start so already-approved config and plugin changes are not silently skipped if the requesting run ends.
  [#201998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201998).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Logs undelivered fallback approval route notices without exposing message content so failed delivery is visible.
  [`70bbddf44c`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/70bbddf44caaf99eec5db0858ab2231e7714acad).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes scheduled automation runs receiving owner-private memory when their requester loses owner status while a triggered run waits for a cron slot.
  [#198697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198697).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).
- Adds an opt-in Tlon setting to keep citation expansion restricted to the source channel, adding remediations for cross-channel context leakage.
  [#187778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187778).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).
- Fixes authenticated CLI-owner tools reporting managed sessions as missing when Gateway roles are enabled.
  [#192573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192573).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Adds opt-in `gateway.repositoryIntegration` enforcement so cloud worker dispatch, checkout, and recovery require valid caller-bound repository admission.
  [#188656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188656).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Fixes profile resolution errors when signing in through Cloudflare Access using an email one-time PIN.
  [#190943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190943).
- Replaces rigid script preflight refusals on chained or piped Python and Node commands with approval-based handling so valid commands are not blocked without recourse.
  [#199590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199590).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Checks execution approval authority once when a request starts so approvals granted during an active run are not refused or dropped at execution time.
  [#201812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201812).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Rejects hidden shell startup environment assignments such as BASH_ENV and ENV passed through inline or nested env -S commands.
  [#203934](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203934).

  Thanks [@pgondhi987](https://github.com/pgondhi987).
- Clarifies Privacy & security settings to label Gateway command defaults accurately and explain how Deny, Auto review, and Full mode handle approval checks.
  [#199441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199441).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Retains admitted continuation source ownership across device changes and reauthorizes fresh starts while preserving model policy and Stop controls.
  [`b5296f4439`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b5296f4439df0775825710eb61849c101e337835).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes `openclaw doctor` reporting a clean device-pairing check when the local device credential store is unreadable or corrupted instead of reporting a finding.
  [#195963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195963).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves per-user delegated Enterprise profiles and exact administrator or session-writing permissions from trusted proxies instead of sharing one native identity.
  [#196856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196856).

  Thanks [@kevinlin-openai](https://github.com/kevinlin-openai).
- Restricts internal agent handoff controls to server-verified local or in-process callers rather than trusting client-declared backend mode.
  [#201845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201845).

  Thanks [@freeqaz](https://github.com/freeqaz).
- Stops automatic reconnect loops after Gateway access is revoked and shows the access-denial recovery screen instead.
  [#197299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197299).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sanitizes peer-supplied client metadata in Gateway connection logs so control characters cannot forge console lines or inject terminal sequences.
  [#191906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191906).

  Thanks [@pgondhi987](https://github.com/pgondhi987).
- Reports the running Gateway's actual network exposure and startup authentication overrides in Privacy & Security, including loopback, all interfaces, Tailscale Serve, and Tailscale Funnel.
  [#191486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191486).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Rejects ambiguous capability-scoped request paths starting with repeated slashes or backslashes with HTTP 400 before routing.
  [#190863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190863).
- Blocks session-only guests from viewing another person's Activity timeline or activity counts while keeping their own activity and shared sessions accessible.
  [#191978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191978).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Removes an unexposed unattended deep-link key from iOS UserDefaults so external deep links cannot bypass confirmation prompts.
  [#197172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197172).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Makes gateway.roles.default optional so Gateways can store role definitions without turning on named-role enforcement for every operator sign-in.
  [#197712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197712).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Preserves Gateway administrator permissions for authenticated admin chat turns sent from Control UI and native apps.
  [`4c363fa0cb`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4c363fa0cb0e45213e5127e37c11333a27df2257).

  Thanks [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Checks forwarded node command approvals once at sanitization so already-approved node commands are not refused mid-dispatch.
  [#201947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201947).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Checks plugin node approval decisions once at request start so started approvals are not refused or dropped partway through.
  [#201968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201968).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes browser notifications using a local profile's permissions instead of the signed-in browser's current OpenClaw ID role and session visibility.
  [#200205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200205).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Adds `openclaw id rebind` so you can move a claw to new endpoint and Gateway keys using its recovery kit while keeping existing people, devices, nodes, and membership history.
  [#196388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196388).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Hardens OpenClaw ID tunnel HTTP framing and WebSocket upgrade routing so requests with unusual headers or paths stay on their intended Gateway route.
  [#196752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196752).

  Thanks [@mmaps](https://github.com/mmaps).
- Stops OpenClaw ID from asking to re-approve an already-approved browser after signing out and signing back in with the same role and scopes.
  [#194354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194354).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Reads OpenClaw ID member roles from gateway.roles.definitions so owners can customize Operator and Viewer permissions or define new roles.
  [#197739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197739).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Lets invited OpenClaw ID members approve and remove their own additional browsers or native app devices while notifying owners and showing device provenance on the People page.
  [#198575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198575).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Adds a guest member role for OpenClaw ID that lets members work in shared sessions with tools kept inside a sandbox.
  [#197748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197748).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Fixes an approved OpenClaw ID member getting refused as already used when reopening their own invite link from the same account and device.
  [#198552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198552).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Lets Gateway owners invite a member to join as an existing person profile on the claw so their name and conversations stay together.
  [#198773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198773).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Fixes Remote access telling owners to remove people or devices when an invitation limit is reached instead of directing them to review the invitation limit.
  [#199266](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199266).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Publishes OpenClaw ID membership changes that arrive while a previous snapshot publication is finishing instead of lagging one update behind.
  [#195261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195261).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Lets Labs-enabled Gateways admit owner-approved node keys over the OpenClaw ID tunnel after six-digit verification and capability review.
  [#194001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194001).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Allows a removed OpenClaw ID node approved with a new invitation to pair automatically once without a second manual approval under Devices.
  [#195507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195507).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Allows linking your OpenClaw ID account to your personal profile under Profile Identity so owner devices connect as you rather than Shared owner.
  [#197628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197628).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Allows admins with OpenClaw ID Labs enabled to turn on remote access over a verified personal sign-in without an SSH tunnel.
  [#198349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198349).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Allows signing in with OpenClaw ID in Control UI over Tailscale or another verified personal sign-in instead of requiring local loopback access.
  [#197559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197559).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Keeps periodic OpenClaw ID snapshot refreshes running after a Gateway restart hits a same-second duplicate timestamp.
  [#194421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194421).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Fixes internal exceptions during plugin access-policy evaluation permanently revoking live operator access and closing the connection.
  [#198245](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198245).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows an explicitly authorized owner administrator to continue a setup request after performing a network lookup instead of blocking subsequent tool calls.
  [#194472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194472).

  Thanks [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal).
- Retains the accepted authorization generation for queued chat inputs until dispatch.
  [`d98df6c24a`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d98df6c24ac2a5b89decf4e1cf0fc1e0a9871e43).
- Keeps an already-started operator request running under its admitted permissions when roles or policies are edited mid-request, applying the new permissions to subsequent requests.
  [#201334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201334).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Enables browser OAuth sign-in behind trusted reverse proxies using gateway.publicOrigin when the proxy rewrites the internal Host header.
  [#195688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195688).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Reauthorizes once at initial runtime admission and reuses that admitted context across retries and fallbacks.
  [`7b0bb487f7`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7b0bb487f7074fef6b2197ff9d615435fe856ce5).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Stops revoked managed-worktree file copy batches from publishing into the destination when caller authority is revoked mid-copy.
  [#196390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196390).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Refuses a CLI turn before launch or first input if its selected saved account is removed while the turn is preparing.
  [#193508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193508).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Clarifies security settings and tool profile preview labels in Control UI without changing underlying permissions.
  [#192264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192264).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Adds an opt-in per-agent default permission mode for new attended sessions and displays the configured default accurately in the New Session picker.
  [#190859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190859).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Enforces session permission modes such as Read only, Ask me, and Auto-review on every Gateway tool call across all runtimes, including owner Claude CLI chats.
  [#202941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202941).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Rejects session invitations targeting channel handles or deleted human identities with `unknown identity` instead of writing ineffective membership rows.
  [#189046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189046).
- Allows guests with `operator.sessions.read` to preview effective tools for conversations they can already open without requiring broad `operator.read` access.
  [#187925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/187925).

  Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Applies each session's resolved sandbox allowlist to tool previews and `/tools` so blocked tools are no longer advertised as available.
  [#188042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188042).

  Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Fixes code-mode and plugin subagents discarding explicit Access choices and inheriting elevated Access from their parent.
  [#197494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197494).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Routes path containment checks across subsystems through one canonical path-resolution owner to prevent symlink spelling mismatches.
  [#195061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195061).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keeps private login email addresses out of shared presence, Activity links, and owner menus when a person has no display name, falling back to their GitHub handle or a neutral label.
  [#191920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191920).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Caps Cloudflare Visitor Access policy responses at 16 MiB and cancels oversized streams early to prevent excessive Gateway memory use.
  [#188959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188959).
- Binds Twilio realtime WebSocket streams to the signature-verified webhook CallSid and rejects webhooks with missing or blank call IDs.
  [#136506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136506).

  Thanks [@drobison00](https://github.com/drobison00).
- Deletes rejected Windows worker GitHub credential profiles from disk immediately instead of leaving them until the turn finishes.
  [#193615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193615).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keeps pending worker creation bound to its original caller across access changes and Gateway restarts.
  [#197875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197875).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Checks saved, queued, and resumed operator requests once against current roles and target agent limits when work starts, avoiding duplicate permission checks during execution.
  [#202513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202513).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes queued, scheduled, and resumed tasks starting after a requester's role was downgraded to read-only by requiring the original creation grant before execution.
  [#203521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203521).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).



#### Other QOL Improvements

This release adds durable background commands that survive Gateway restarts, faster queued-message steering, and streamlined repository worktree and GitHub PR workflows. It also improves PostgreSQL and Gateway responsiveness across long-running sessions.


##### Durable background commands and chat steering


- Keeps durable background `exec` processes running across Gateway restarts on Linux and macOS, and writes background `exec` output to a file named in the exit wake.
  [#188465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188465).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#197474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197474).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).

  [#199992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199992).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Lets you send pending steers immediately without stopping the active run, edit queued messages, and get rejected sends back in the composer.
  [#193665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193665).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#197908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197908).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Adds required manual-step handoffs directly in chat, lets `sessions_spawn` target a specific paired device, lets agents inspect generated images before delivery, and automatically expires disposable agent scratch directories after 24 hours.
  [#196951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196951).

  Thanks [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal) and [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).

  [#197469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197469).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs).

  [#198227](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198227).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#193677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193677).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).




##### Repository worktrees and GitHub PR workflows


- Hands repository disk custody to Gateway worktrees and consumes asynchronously prepared checkouts while speeding up hydration, branch refreshes, dependency preparation, and recovery snapshots in large or busy repositories.
  [#189350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189350).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).

  [#188370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188370).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#191135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191135).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#194712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194712), [#195228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195228), [#195252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195252), [#196886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196886), [#198414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198414), [#199075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199075).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Lets you edit verified pull requests and request PR re-reviews without importing source, updates an existing PR when publishing a checkout again, supports configurable remote repository session branch prefixes, and reduces publication option delays.
  [#188904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188904), [#189006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189006).

  Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).

  [#199525](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199525).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  [#188648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188648).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).

  [#188414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188414).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).




##### PostgreSQL and Gateway responsiveness


- Holds Gateway state ownership directly in PostgreSQL on all-PostgreSQL installs, reuses prepared statements, reads each chat history page from one database snapshot, and bounds PostgreSQL search snippet and maintenance age work.
  [#196237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196237), [#184013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184013), [#190001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190001), [#190309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190309).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).

  [#192849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192849).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reduces Gateway startup module-file overhead, speeds up multi-agent config reads and archive metadata listings, cuts streaming broadcast and compaction rewrite stalls, and accepts co-author credit from trusted-proxy headers.
  [#192217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192217).

  Thanks [@karkarl](https://github.com/karkarl).

  [#193841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193841).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).

  [#192732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192732).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  [#201420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201420), [#202547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202547).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#196867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196867).

  Thanks [@russellb](https://github.com/russellb).




##### Additional quality-of-life improvements


- Allows agents to perform explicitly authorized non-disruptive component builds and dependency repairs on the hosting Gateway under normal approvals.
  [#198396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198396).

  Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Makes waiting background process polls return immediately when output arrives or a command finishes instead of waiting for a 250 ms polling tick.
  [#189491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189491).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Batches background command completions that finish while an earlier completion turn is still queued or running into a single agent turn instead of waking the agent once per command.
  [#199113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199113).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reduces Gateway event-loop stalls during chat turn preparation by making TTS preference reads asynchronous and avoiding redundant filesystem probes.
  [#191075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191075).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shows specific argument validation messages on stderr when openclaw agent fails in JSON mode with blank target selectors.
  [#199641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199641).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Shows argument validation errors on stderr when openclaw system event fails in JSON mode.
  [#199670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199670).

  Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668).
- Avoids starting configured plugin runtimes when printing root CLI help or version output.
  [#191681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191681).

  Thanks [@baisiqi6](https://github.com/baisiqi6).
- Separates native worker stderr diagnostics from active CLI progress lines so output does not overwrite or garble progress.
  [`de01cd6afb`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/de01cd6afbb03b61287ba905598c5338428a840f).

  Thanks [@daniel-lxs](https://github.com/daniel-lxs) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Reuses compatible ready cloud workers across repository commits instead of discarding them for a cold setup.
  [#193667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193667).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces remote workspace verification scans during cloud repository session startup from three to one.
  [#193774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193774).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses unchanged files, hashes, toolchains, and dependencies when refreshing prepared workers to a nearby commit.
  [#193784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193784).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Combines remote transfer and setup commands when refreshing prepared cloud workers to reduce round trips.
  [#193901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193901).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Adds an opt-in mode for repository-only cloud sessions to keep their original attached disk authoritative between turns instead of copying checkpoints to the Gateway after every turn.
  [#189668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189668).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Keeps Code Mode session-store reads responsive in long conversations by returning only changed values instead of replaying full history on each turn.
  [#195878](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195878).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids repeated serialization stalls when Code Mode reloads a long-lived session store by compacting superseded writes during replay.
  [#194769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194769).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explains how to invoke deferred tools through dispatch_action when a direct call is attempted instead of returning a generic not-found error.
  [`2e385a6153`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2e385a61530fead8a21fffa954b60d0897d2dd87).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes high-scale diff PNG exports failing when the image can fit within the pixel limit by automatically downscaling.
  [#197054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197054).

  Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Reduces main-thread stalls when file edits or missing-file reads compute diagnostic path suggestions in large directories.
  [#194790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194790).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gives root-aware recovery guidance when host file tools reject a path in another repository outside the recorded session root.
  [#194473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194473).

  Thanks [@jason-allen-oneal](https://github.com/jason-allen-oneal).
- Logs request failures from already-disconnected WebSocket clients at debug level to cut down log noise.
  [`2b085aeef9`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2b085aeef9608a96f338718b6f715ee05e6e1c35).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Reduces Gateway memory usage across many active sessions by sharing published tool checkpoint descriptors.
  [#199609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199609).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows more active session replicas to fit within the existing memory budget by counting shared skills snapshots once.
  [#199748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199748).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Releases diagnostic activity state when sessions are reset or deleted instead of retaining it until a Gateway restart.
  [#199761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199761).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces SQLite worker startup time and memory footprint by avoiding unused PostgreSQL schema loading.
  [#200066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200066).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reduces memory retained after Gateway restarts by releasing prepared restart-recovery rows once recovery completes.
  [#200435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200435).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces memory allocation and internal worker traffic during session appends when the transcript cache is under pressure.
  [#199615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199615).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces worker-to-main copying during small session appends on busy Gateways after session replicas are evicted.
  [#199673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199673).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces memory usage and worker startup overhead on long-running Gateways with many SQLite write scopes.
  [#199901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199901).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Advertises the chat input custody contract in the Gateway handshake so clients can detect support.
  [#180651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/180651).

  Thanks [@bkudiess](https://github.com/bkudiess).
- Batches historical blob fetches during agent Git history reads in shared blobless worktrees to prevent tiny-pack storms.
  [#197259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197259).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Batches content searches across shared blobless Git history to avoid thousands of lazy fetches and tiny promisor packs.
  [#197448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197448).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes goals displaying Paused after Stop when a run was interrupted automatically rather than stopped by you.
  [#198276](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198276).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Stops agents from asking for unnecessary Goal Resume handoffs when the user has already authorized the work and the Goal is inactive.
  [#198722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198722).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Allows starting a new goal immediately after the previous goal in a session is achieved without manually clearing or resuming the completed goal.
  [#199449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199449).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Fixes HTML session exports showing answers without their questions when older transcripts omit parent links.
  [#197310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197310).

  Thanks [@Jo2234](https://github.com/Jo2234).
- Raises the default Linux delegated agent-exec cgroup IO bandwidth ceiling on non-rotational disks from 160 MiB/s to 250 MiB/s per direction while preserving Gateway responsiveness.
  [#189742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189742).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Allows large Linux host commands and builds to use unused shared memory capacity automatically instead of stopping at a fixed per-command quarter-RAM cap.
  [#193044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193044).

  Thanks [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Stops macOS SQLite opens from spawning a mount subprocess on local APFS storage, speeding up Gateway boot and database access.
  [#193859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193859).

  Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Prevents Git pack maintenance from stalling managed worktree base refreshes during repository fetches.
  [#191152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191152).

  Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Reduces stalls when opening managed session diffs after file timestamp changes that did not alter tracked file contents.
  [#188781](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188781).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Moves `.worktreeinclude` file copying off the Gateway main thread so other sessions stay responsive during large managed-worktree provisioning.
  [#191873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191873).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces stalls when removing clean managed worktrees in large repositories by retaining HEAD directly instead of building a redundant recovery commit.
  [#199777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199777).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps read-only agent Git commit existence probes offline in partial clones so missing-commit checks do not trigger multi-minute history downloads.
  [#195498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195498).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Publishes finished Linux Podman dependency templates immediately once builder processes exit instead of waiting on container removal cleanup.
  [#192411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192411).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses discovered Podman runtime details across sandbox preparations to avoid startup stalls while Podman cleans up unrelated containers.
  [#192872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192872).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates PostgreSQL connection help text to describe supported environment, file, exec SecretRef, and TLS options accurately.
  [#192194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192194).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Reuses prepared sandbox dependencies across up to four recently used commits so concurrent sessions do not repeatedly rebuild a single shared template.
  [#195883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195883).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces sandbox preparation time by avoiding full-checkout rehashing when reusing an unchanged template or advancing only a few files.
  [#196044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196044).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Speeds up provisioning of large ignored directories in Linux managed worktrees by copying files in native reflink batches instead of repeating per-file checks.
  [#196298](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196298).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resumes btrfs-backed sandbox containers right after taking an atomic snapshot so long workspace discovery and Git reconciliation no longer keep guest processes frozen.
  [#196662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196662).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids full btrfs snapshot overhead for fast checkpoints on small sandbox workspaces while keeping snapshot capture for larger workspaces.
  [#196727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196727).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents full-history downloads when an agent runs Git content commands against a commit missing from a shared blobless clone by negotiating missing commits first.
  [#196743](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196743).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps managed-worktree dependency copies fast even when a long-running Gateway has a large resident memory footprint.
  [#196768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196768).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents `git rev-parse` content revision expressions from triggering full-history promisor downloads in shared blobless clones.
  [#196784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196784).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shortens sandbox container freezes during workspace reconciliation by keeping the guest running while immutable patch and staging work is prepared.
  [#196835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196835).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves partial-clone promisor filtering across publication, pinned-commit, and secondary-repository fetches so shared blobless clones do not accumulate bloated packs.
  [#196854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196854).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids full workspace content rehashing after sandbox exports or host imports by preserving unchanged file fingerprints across both sides.
  [#196937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196937).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skips redundant validation of immutable sandbox template generations and unnecessary Git delta searches during template refreshes to speed up session startup after commit changes.
  [#196986](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196986).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses the validated Podman target and container ID on prepared sandboxes instead of running engine discovery before every command.
  [#191574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191574).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps sandbox guests running during GitHub history and object downloads instead of freezing the sandbox.
  [#197243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197243).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prepares all sandbox runtime targets before pausing any guest and logs detailed freeze phases to shorten multi-writer pauses.
  [#197347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197347).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps sandboxed agents running during atomic btrfs workspace snapshots instead of freezing the container after commands finish.
  [#198128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198128).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prewarms sandbox dependency templates between sessions after lockfile changes so the next sandboxed session starts much faster.
  [#197953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197953).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses prepared workspace manifest baselines and synchronized skills across repeated sandbox turns when files have not changed.
  [#191057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191057).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces wait time and duplicate filesystem work when concurrent turns prepare the same local sandbox workspace.
  [#189299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189299).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Speeds up acceptance of large local sandbox workspaces by reusing saved workspace fingerprints from recovery checkpoints.
  [#199683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199683).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids an unnecessary full workspace rehash the first time a newly initialized local sandbox workspace is reused unchanged.
  [#199687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199687).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compresses large workspace reconciliation checkpoints sent to the database worker, reducing transfer sizes for large workspaces.
  [#199794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199794).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces memory allocation during periodic reconciliation of large local workspaces.
  [#200094](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200094).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids rescanning unchanged large tool outputs during long tool loops, reducing request-preparation CPU and memory use.
  [#191378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191378).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlaps independent reads during agent turn preparation, health checks, Slack history loading, and cron refreshes to reduce avoidable waiting.
  [#191749](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191749).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Avoids unnecessary transcript copying on the main thread when preparing manual session compaction.
  [#199840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199840).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), and [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Adds an atomic `sessions.groups.create` Gateway API so API clients can create empty session groups without overwriting groups created concurrently by other clients.
  [#186896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/186896).

  Thanks [@bkudiess](https://github.com/bkudiess).
- Avoids scanning unrelated and retired agent stores when renaming or deleting a small session group.
  [#184472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/184472).

  Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).
- Selects matching run-input records before decoding unrelated transcript payloads to speed up session history lookups.
  [`fdaf28dd4e`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/fdaf28dd4e093ce4960251f2c2a868f63a7ef5ac).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Keeps automatic utility-model observer failures in Gateway logs instead of posting warnings into chats when no utility model was explicitly configured.
  [#193657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193657).

  Thanks [@sallyom](https://github.com/sallyom).
- Avoids redundant SQLite hydration and accounting reads when unchanged sessions are read concurrently or repeatedly.
  [#191100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191100).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps your custom session icon and color after running /reset instead of clearing them.
  [#138334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138334).

  Thanks [@teddytennant](https://github.com/teddytennant).
- Guides agents to reuse existing workspace repository checkouts or shared-object worktrees instead of creating duplicate clones.
  [#197913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197913).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reuses attested file hashes when binding snapshot-restored workspaces instead of rehashing every unchanged file.
  [#193662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193662).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skips redundant full git fsck passes on completed worker object stores and reports separate preparation timings for warm images.
  [#193756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193756).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Logs the specific runtime rejection or withdrawal cause when steering falls back to a queued turn.
  [#197415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197415).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves the underlying runtime reason when a steer expires behind a long tool call and falls back to a follow-up.
  [#197856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197856).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes Template review and publishing failing when the display name or description has leading or trailing spaces.
  [#197979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197979).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Creates the agent and durable chat immediately when adding a Template so you have a place to draft and retry while verified setup completes.
  [#196392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196392).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Shows all questions and numbered choices inside ask_user tool cards in the terminal UI.
  [#190431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190431).

  Thanks [@mengyuyuan](https://github.com/mengyuyuan).
- Adds an opt-in Labs setting that prioritizes frequently and recently used tools in the Code Mode index and Tool Search directory.
  [#197128](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197128).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Makes tool validation errors and truncated tool output messages more actionable while preserving text alongside retained images.
  [`a33f6740a2`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a33f6740a20f33da866de4160b69203567ac6f12).

  Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes main-thread stalls during tool-heavy turns by updating only the affected tool ancestry instead of rebuilding the whole presentation graph.
  [#198064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198064).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Speeds up detailed session usage queries and reuses unchanged cached usage reports instead of recomputing them every 30 seconds.
  [#201785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201785).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps the Gateway responsive when validating large widget scripts by running syntax validation in a worker and reusing results for unchanged scripts.
  [#189903](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189903).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps unchanged sandbox workspaces warm between consecutive turns instead of pausing and resuming the sandbox on every turn.
  [#188832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188832).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eliminates long pauses after Git finishes downloading workspace objects in fragmented partial clones.
  [#197081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197081).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents workspace preparation from re-downloading Git object batches that another preparation has already completed.
  [#197251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197251).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Includes a persistent diagnostic ID on failed managed workspace preparations so operators can match failures to the underlying Git output.
  [#197862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197862).

  Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Explains managed repository refresh failures in plain language with clear next steps alongside the diagnostic ID.
  [#198444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198444).

  Thanks [@anmol0b](https://github.com/anmol0b).
- Reduces CPU usage during cold concurrent sandbox and worktree preparation.
  [#193211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193211).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).



#### Other Bug Fixes

This release resolves agent deletion races, restart and recovery gaps for interrupted work and background replies, and long-session compaction and formatting issues. It also fixes durable `exec` and sandbox edge cases alongside CLI interruption and completion bugs.


##### Agent deletion, startup, and state reliability


- Fixes agent deletion failing when personal pins change mid-operation, finishes interrupted agent deletions after a restart without failing readiness, and prevents retained deleted agents from blocking Gateway startup or startup schema upgrades.
  [#203076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203076).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#194584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194584).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#200284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200284).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), and [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).

  [`f2fc41ab59`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f2fc41ab59dce7f59e1730ac7b00fa602a205cf2), [`e061ec63a7`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e061ec63a7ed6267603e8ae14ca863b2f4ffbf5a).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes Gateway restarts during startup failing with a state database error, prevents first chats from failing during cold storage startup or missing initial databases, clears stale cold-session markers after restart, stops long Bun transcripts from stalling or crash-looping the Gateway, and reduces Linux allocator fragmentation.
  [#203530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203530).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#199565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199565).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#199862](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199862), [#201534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201534).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#200718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200718).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  [#202407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202407).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Cleans up leftover SQLite inspection snapshot copies across cache namespaces and timed-out or interrupted triage runs, releases snapshot disk space after cleanup, and prevents stale SQLite query plans as agent databases grow.
  [#193174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193174), [#193652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193652), [#193656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193656).

  [#201661](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201661).

  [#199403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199403).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps PostgreSQL leases alive during lock contention, bounds silent PostgreSQL requests by the maintenance deadline, supports porting and backing up large plugin blobs, lets fresh PostgreSQL installs port back to SQLite, preserves numeric scores in session recall, and stops marking data at risk when a rollback has no baseline.
  [#201853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201853).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

  [`e4a5df09ad`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e4a5df09ad8e1ccd2263405301c813ba0b5dd289).

##### Interrupted work, subagent results, and restart recovery


- Retains owed subagent results across Gateway restarts, resumes yielded parent sessions after completion or upgrade recovery, retries completion delivery after requester interruption, and keeps child follow-up results valid across requester lifecycle changes.
  [`025a940856`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/025a9408567a9e1fcaede7960ebf6954af45e163), [`3dd3b7f194`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3dd3b7f194774e59941e35415a408aa6e35688ba).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#199488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199488), [#199868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199868), [#198640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198640), [#200165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200165), [#200581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200581), [#201706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201706), [#202338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202338), [#202647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202647).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stops losing finished or failed subagent results during access-policy outages, steer races, expiry, closed plugin invocation scopes, or premature subagent chat deletion, and keeps detached tool runs from dropping their final reply.
  [#197658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197658), [#200649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200649), [#200760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200760), [#196940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196940), [#199550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199550).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  [#198732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/198732).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#193869](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193869).

  Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored).

  [#189984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/189984).

  Thanks [@ethanhawkes-gif](https://github.com/ethanhawkes-gif).
- Recovers automatically when the Gateway main thread wedges, diagnoses wedged Gateways and resumes sessions after `SIGKILL`, preserves interrupted turns and streamed replies across restarts or shutdown overruns, resumes running channel threads and direct chat turns in retained tasks, and preserves requester permissions when interrupted work resumes.
  [#194481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194481), [#190964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190964), [#192501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192501), [#188402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188402), [#195164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195164), [#199148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199148).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#200394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200394).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

  [#201418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201418).

  Thanks [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186).

- Fixes graceful Gateway shutdown outliving its stop deadline, exits promptly after clean shutdown and plugin scope retirement, allows restarts after late sidecar cleanup, and finishes database cleanup after slow shutdown steps.

##### Sessions, compaction, streaming, and GitHub workflows


- Fixes `dispatch_action` reporting a delivered `sessions_send` as failed in child-report turns, drains passive notices on `sessions_send` resumes, keeps yielded subagent command wakes on their task, accepts follow-up messages right after a run finishes without appearing to restart work, and fixes missing final replies after tool calls or queued ACP prompts.
  [#196034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196034).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).

##### Exec, sandboxes, and node runtime


- Fixes adopted durable `exec` commands claiming no exit wake after a Gateway restart, follows chat keys for restart completion wakes, keeps adopted commands tracked if process inspection fails, counts only real services against the `exec` service cap, preserves foreground `exec` output, stops progress refreshes from stalling during background commands, keeps line-continued commands intact during shell preflight, and keeps `pathPrepend` warnings on finished `host=node` results.

##### Agent runs, sessions, and compaction fixes


- Fixes active sessions running a tool being reported as idle in logs and diagnostics after a steering message dispatches or queues.
  [#194907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194907).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shows `<root>` instead of a blank path when openclaw agent --config-override rejects an unrecognized top-level key.
  [#193142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193142).

  Thanks [@NotAFlightRisk](https://github.com/NotAFlightRisk).
- Prevents unsafe model fallback after a CLI agent run fails when required backend cleanup also fails.
  [#191859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191859).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes final-answer recovery after tool-only turns crashing with an invalid time value error when a context engine omits message timestamps.
  [#201766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201766).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Fixes the file edit tool rejecting valid replacements when the matched text consists only of whitespace or blank lines.
  [#190039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190039).

  Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Stops subagent completion announcements from retrying indefinitely after the requester session is archived.
  [#195258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195258).
- Fixes yielded worktree sessions failing to wake when a background command from their first turn exits.
  [#203113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203113).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Prevents adding or editing an unrelated agent from interrupting in-flight background command completions.
  [#195615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195615).

  Thanks [@saariuslystoned](https://github.com/saariuslystoned), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), and [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keeps agent-detached and background service processes alive across completed host exec calls and one-shot local runs while keeping them tracked and bounded.
  [#199079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199079).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Labels background run results as user-side input rather than assistant replies in model context so agents do not confuse them with their own turns.
  [`e57945151e`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e57945151e15d533726b41b69c4230e27394b3ae).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes background services started by an agent disconnecting when they write log output after the originating command finishes.
  [#200412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200412).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes editing Gateway configuration aborting in-progress subagent resumes and background command follow-up turns.
  [#200516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200516).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Fixes archived usage reads potentially selecting the wrong agent's chat history when a saved path uses an indirect relative symlink.
  [#200505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200505).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Delivers the final reply from a CLI-backed agent even after it sent an earlier progress message during the same turn.
  [#188900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188900).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Fixes restricted CLI completion turns advertising an incomplete exec tool schema without background or timeout parameters.
  [#197759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197759).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus) and [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

  [#199369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199369).

  Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Fixes `openclaw sessions compact --agent " " --json` hiding the blank-agent error behind a generic CLI failure message on stderr.
  [#199386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199386).

  Thanks [@shuisadev](https://github.com/shuisadev).
- Keeps probe durations, cache windows, and context-breakdown retries anchored to elapsed time across host wall-clock changes.
  [#190894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190894).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

  Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt).

  [#196080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196080).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).

  Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Raises the Linux agent-exec I/O budget on solid-state storage to 160 MiB/s and 40,960 IOPS per direction so commands are no longer heavily throttled.
  [#188894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188894).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps Gateway-owned managed worktree and Git operations outside the Linux agent-exec cgroup limits so chat startup and Git RPCs do not time out.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents cold-loading Code Mode state in long sessions from exhausting worker memory by folding saved snapshots incrementally.

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Loads `AGENTS.md`, `SOUL.md`, and other bootstrap files from each recorded worktree session's own workspace instead of the shared agent checkout.

  [#192469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192469).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Keeps paused subagents alive across unrelated requester notices and closes their message waits reliably when the owning requester task finishes.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes the process log tool falsely reporting no output recorded when called with a zero or negative limit.

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
