- Fixes interrupted sessions stalling after a Gateway restart when a fresh event arrives before recovery input is admitted.
  [#197668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/197668).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes sandbox templates losing their last-good prepared dependencies when a graceful Gateway shutdown interrupts validation or incremental refresh.
  [#195713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195713).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps managed-worktree default-base fetches from blocking other worktree registrations when origin defines extra fetch mappings.
  [#195892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195892).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes worktree preparation stalling behind background Git pack consolidation so foreground worktrees can be prepared while pack maintenance runs.
  [#195968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195968).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves the underlying failing phase in workspace preparation diagnostics when a state lease is cancelled and includes explicit retry guidance for expired or replaced leases.
  [#196739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196739).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Includes redacted install diagnostics when sandbox dependency installation fails and excludes task-local pnpm stores from captured workspace results.
  [#192051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192051).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Removes newly created Docker or Podman sandbox containers and their registry entries when backend preparation fails after container startup.
  [#191555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/191555).

  Thanks [@aishwary-dongre](https://github.com/aishwary-dongre).
- Fixes guest sandbox turns getting stuck behind workspace recovery after downloading large managed tools.
  [#190178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190178).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shortens pauses for running sandbox commands during workspace base refreshes and PR source preparation.
  [#199717](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199717).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes workspace reconciliation retries writing redundant recovery journals when applicable edits are already in place.
  [#200057](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200057).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Excludes re-downloadable NuGet and Convex caches from cloud workspace results so reconciliation does not fail for oversized outputs.
  [#202327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202327).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matches canonically equivalent Unicode names in Signal alias directory lookups, command-palette catalog searches, and local Codex history searches.
  [#188138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188138).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Cancels in-progress standalone approval client startup promptly when stopping so shutdown does not wait on an unfinished handshake.
  [#195700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195700).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Fixes local media containment checks rejecting valid missing or nested paths inside symlinked state directories.
  [#195009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/195009).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Skips the macOS-only Tailscale.app lookup on Linux and Windows when no Tailscale CLI is installed.
  [#190767](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190767).
- Routes CLI Template additions through the running Gateway state owner for exact-version review, consent, and apply while keeping local offline fallback for local paths.
  [#196431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196431).

  Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Fixes failed Template Add cleanup being unable to inspect existing package owners when shared state is stored in PostgreSQL.
  [#196793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/196793).

  Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Keeps local wait budgets accurate across system clock changes for command follow-ups, runtime auth, process exits, scheduled task settlement, and update handoffs.
  [#199951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/199951).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Avoids scanning SQLite transcript history during usage refresh when an archived transcript cannot be expanded.
  [#192270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192270).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes warm-image capture aborting when a recorded worker process has already exited or its PID was reused.
  [#193653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193653).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes managed local workspace preparation failing during Git initialization on native Windows.
  [#190281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190281).

  Thanks [@HootRock](https://github.com/HootRock).
- Honors explicitly selected npm and npx executables or wrappers on Windows instead of silently switching to bundled npm.
  [#190745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/190745).

  Thanks [@AmelioMansour](https://github.com/AmelioMansour).
- Preserves the original missing-module error when a native worker fails to start instead of replacing it with DataCloneError.
  [#194935](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/194935).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes workers running setup scripts even when the selected profile explicitly disables setup scripts.
  [#192538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192538).

  Thanks [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Rejects truncated Git index output during worker workspace transfers and preserves the previous workspace instead of consuming an incomplete index.
  [#192571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/192571).

  Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen).
- Prevents pnpm fallback stores created under `.openclaw/.pnpm-store` in managed guest shells from syncing back into projects as authored workspace files.
  [#188089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/188089).

  Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Preserves rollback custody when transferring local workspace publication packs.
  [`a92f28501a`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a92f28501ab89b46c6e6816f306d3e3eded9a040).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Displays specific worktree and Gateway client port validation messages instead of hiding them behind a generic CLI failure.
  [#193005](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193005).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Prevents background repository maintenance from inflating Git pack sizes in shared partial-clone worktrees.
  [#193361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/193361).

  Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fixes already-started node commands failing partway through with an authority-closed error when the calling run ends before completion.
  [#201887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201887).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes agent tools discarding the real result of an in-process Gateway call when the run ends or the Gateway reloads before the call returns.
  [#202036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/202036).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes duplicate session title updates in the runtime.
  [`0d807d940b`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0d807d940b43176e2400e1db69dabb5c5cd5bb36).

  Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman) and [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Preserves the original requester's current access when delivering a pending subagent result after a Gateway restart, preventing dropped deliveries or lost admin permissions.
  [#203479](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203479).

  Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fixes replayed subagent settlement notices losing their original privacy classification after a restart or replay.
  [`d37c8a9dee`](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d37c8a9dee58ad2dfccc6d7db9f284d156022018).

  Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
